Scene: in the step-2 package gate below (no demo calls it every tick yet; the demos cache the last direction in a field instead).

**Step-2 engine package, Predator gate (2026-10-22):** NEUTRAL, kept as hygiene. `runPredatorAbab --vs HEAD`, headed, 4 pairs: pins honoured by collision callbacks and messages (N-56), blob loader (N-57), setAnimation (N-58), hot-swap field check (N-59), page instances frozen (N-60). logic 4.277 → 4.359 ms +2.2 % (2/4 worse, cv 7/8 %), spatial −4.1 %, physics −7.1 %, renderer −3.0 %, preRender −3.6 % (0/4); load keys within 0.5 %.

**Boid facing through setRotationCS, N-41 (2026-10-21):** kept (`c750a095`), hygiene. `this.setRotationCS(c, s)` in place of a direct `enqueueSetRotCS`. Kernel (scratch, 512 bodies a batch, ring drained after each, normal compile): 89–90 → 92 ns per call, +2.5 %, worse in 4–5 of 7 rounds. Boids scene, 4 ABAB pairs against HEAD, headless: each logic worker 1.2 ms, under the 3 ms floor, so screening only. Logic summed +11 / +4.8 / −0.7 / +1.3 %; logic0 alone +19 % (3/4), with ~1.6 ms spikes on both sides. Physics, pre-render and spatial, which it cannot reach, read 3.6–11 % better on the same side.

**Planet water pull as one host setting, N-17 (2026-10-21):** kept (`da9dfe76`, `ddb7efc9`), a bug fix measured for cost. `LiquidFun.setRadialGravity` replaces a ring command per particle per logic tick; the host adds accel × dt to each particle's velocity before each step. Planet scene, 3769 particles poured by 120 `q` taps, 12 one-second samples after 4 s of settling, 3 ABAB pairs, headless. To keep the water the same on both sides the tree ran the pull at G/2, HEAD's effective pull (LiquidFun drains `applyForce` in the first of 2 sub-steps; median water height 31.6–33.6 px on HEAD, 33.8–34.0 on the tree). Physics +3.8 / −5.7 / −6.2 % (2/3 better); logic, both workers, 0.87 → 0.10 ms (under the floor, screening only). HEAD dropped ring commands in every run, the tree in none. With the full G as shipped: water 25 px high, physics 13.9 ms in one run (HEAD 12.4–13.3), the denser water's cost. Scratch probe, not a committed tool.

**Several physics steps for a long frame, B7 (2026-10-21):** kept (`37827604`), correctness. Balls on the demo page, physics step per frame: Time.scale 1 6.3 → 6.6 ms, scale 3 7.0 → 11.5 ms. Scale 3 is now two steps, so physics does twice the work it skipped before; one sitting, single medians.

**Short burst tail for a few sync casts, E-57 (2026-10-21):** dropped. Short spin until a burst has served 16 requests: 8 casts per tick physics step+wake −38 % (6/6), logic0 +40 % (5/6); 384-cast stream logic0 +3.1 % (4/6). The tail only moves the wait between physics and logic. Serving pending queries between physics sub-steps is the shape left to try.

**Spatial neighbour gather in cell order, E-70 + E-75 (2026-10-21):** dropped, all slower in the spatial kernel (checksums equal): per-worker per-frame cell copy −10 % (3.3 reads per copied cell), shared slot-parallel positions −5.2 % (4.7 MB in order beats nothing; `entityPosData` is 264 KB and cached), branchless candidate append −4.3 %. The "25 % on the gather" in a Chrome profile was latency charged to a load, not a layout problem.

**Sweep event directions, E-63 (2026-10-21):** kept (`fd15ae95`). The sweep took `Math.cos/sin` of every event angle that `atan2` had just made from a vertex; the direction is now stored with the event. Not bit for bit, so a frozen pre-change sweep and H-06's 1e-4 gate: 3000 mixed scenes, max move 1.2e-10, same counts. Kernel +16.6 % (49/49); VisPolyStressScene pre-render −9.0 % (5/6), CompoundVisPoly `boxes=100` −3.9 % (5/6); the renderer's +5–8 % lean is inside an A/A spread of −9.5 to +35.8 %. **E-74** (`rayPolyDist` divides only for crossed edges): +2.3 % (33/49), dropped.

**Water tunnelling into the planet, N-18 (2026-10-21):** closed, probe artifact (`59d9cd6d`). The probe bound 64 body slots for 310 terrain islands; 246 islands had no shapes. With a slot per island, 0 particles get in at any drop with the planet's skin 0. The earlier "skin does not matter" reading came from the same broken world; on the fixed one a 1 px skin lets 3–13 particles 15–42 px in, so the planet's skin 0 is the right setting.

**Self-lit follows visibility, N-19 (2026-10-21):** fixed (`ed5a7b89`), a bug fix measured for cost. One owner store per improved polar bin. Read from a module-level `let`, the owner cost the compound collect kernel 8–11 % (island 104k → 93k passes/s, 0/49), with the stamping chain still inlined (`--trace-turbo-inlining`). As a parameter down `collectSolidSegments` → `rasterPolarSpan` → `stampPolarBin`: −7.7 %. Setting the `_polarActive` module flag once per span (from the bins' return values) instead of in every improved bin: −5.1 % island, −0.5 % large. Lesson: in a hot inlined loop, a module variable read or written per iteration is a context-slot access TurboFan does not keep in a register. Compound scene `ringSegments=64`, 6 pairs: pre-render +1.7 % (4/6), renderer −4.5 % (5/6 better, plausibly fewer self-lit fills).

**Typed start for the other workers' fields, E-73 (2026-10-21):** screened, no change. Spatial's 15 fields +0.6 % (35/49), pre-render's collector columns +0.7 % (25/49): ties. The pose columns' upper bound is +9.6 % (58/81) on a collect with poses published, but `_latchPose` resets them to `null` every frame as the "no pose" sign, so the change is a new sentinel at ~15 readers for ~0.1 ms of pre-render: parked. The cost appears where per-entity code tests the array's truthiness (emit's guards, `_displayPose`), not where a loop only indexes a hoisted local.

**Render-queue columns typed from the constructor, E-71 + E-72 (2026-10-21):** kept (`a49482f4`, `4289cbc5`). A field declared `null` and later given a typed array has field type Any in V8 (`--trace-generalization`: `h{Any}`); declared as an empty array of the right class it keeps `h{Class(…)}`. N-32's 8 `null` column declarations cost the emit kernel 15 %; the other 16 columns had been `null` all along. Emit kernel 1,458 → 1,717 (E-71, +17.7 %) → 2,004 ops/s (E-72, +17.8 %), 49/49 each, checksums equal. Predator headless 8 pairs, pre-render at matched load +53 µs (se 154) and +12 µs (se 186): emit is under 1 ms of 6.7 ms there. A/A of the E-72 sitting: worker medians −4.6 to +7.2 % with identical code. Audit for the other workers: E-73.

**Component getter by integer slot, E-69 (2026-10-21):** dropped. Replacing `_componentCache[name]` (megamorphic keyed load in the one shared getter) with a closure-captured slot into an array: kernel −12.7 % holey, tie when pre-filled; Predator 8 pairs logic −137 µs (se 70) at matched load, but the getter's own CPU-profile time **749 → 1,079 ms**. Restored. See L4 (2026-09-30), the same area: kernel wins do not carry to Predator's ~15 classes.

**Pre-render boxing in Chrome, E-66 (2026-10-21):** kept (`fbaaad81`, `878bc55a`, `7b980c2f`). Sampling heap profile of Predator: pre-render allocated 30.1 MB/s (Node: nothing on the same paths). Light comparators returning `a − b` (3.5 MB/s) now return a sign; the per-entity pose scratch object (17.6 MB/s, x and y most likely tagged fields through shared map transitions) is a Float64Array; the returned sort key (8.9 MB/s) is stored into its slot. Pre-render allocation → 0.27 MB/s. Predator: **pre-render −8.7 % (4/4)** for the pose scratch; the comparators −3.7 % (3/4); the sort key a tie over 10 pairs.

**Contact begin Set, E-54 (2026-10-21):** kept (`1a1c4c37`). The old premise (0 % of Predator's keys box) used 16,583 ids; Predator now has 42,633 slots and its soldiers, trees and rocks are above 16384. The stay loop's per-pair key + Set probe was 4.2 MB/s in one logic worker; a per-slot stamp replaces it (parity test against the Set reference). Predator: **logic −382 µs at matched load (se 132 µs)**, about −9.5 %.

**FSM time boxed per onUpdate, E-68 (2026-10-21):** screened. One heap number per FSM tick (0.8 MB/s per logic worker in Predator, ~20 µs a frame). Needs a public signature change.

**Merge binary searches "not inlined", E-64 (2026-10-21):** screened, premise wrong, reverted. A Chrome profile listed `firstRayAfter` / `firstRayAtOrAfter` as frames, read as un-inlined calls boxing a double. One call site each tied in Chrome (merge inclusive 233 → 230 ms) and lost 5.4 % in the Node kernel. V8's CPU profiler names inlined code by its source function, so a frame in the profile is not evidence of a call: ask `--trace-turbo-inlining`.

**Merge arcs without atan2, E-62 (2026-10-21):** kept (`483f1da7`). E-43's `mergeSweep` spent 63 % of its time on two `atan2` per sweep edge, used only to choose candidate rays. The cross product of the slack-extended ends now picks the short way and `pseudoAngle` (`y/(|x|+|y|)` by quadrant, slope 1/2 to 1 per radian, so the 1e-6 pad still covers 1e-6 rad) orders ends and rays. Output still comes only from `raySegT`: bit-identical. Kernel, 7 rounds, checksums equal: merge +13.5 %, placeholder circle +6.2 % / +10.5 %. Compound scene `boxes=100`, CPU profiles of 7.6 s with the same total busy time: `mergeSweep` inclusive time **346 → 233 ms (−33 %)**, about 0.25 ms a frame. Its `STEP_MS`, 6 ABAB pairs: −0.5 % (4/6), a tie; the scene's run-to-run load spread (±10 %) hides ~6 %. At `boxes=60` (−7.5 %, 5/6) two tree runs fell under the 3 ms floor, so that row fails. Kept on the kernel; the scene is not worse. (The commit message's −60 % is `mergeSweep`'s self time alone, without its inlined callees.)

**Chained outline raster, E-61 (2026-10-21):** kept (`0b8b07f2`). A CPU profile after E-43 put `writePolarSegment` at 27 % of pre-render busy, 90 % of it two `atan2` per edge. Pre-render walks each outline in a chain order (built on first use after a rebuild; `seg` keeps its order for Pixi) and reuses the angle of the end it just rastered when the next edge starts there; bit-identical (±0, the −π fold and the zero-length case argued in the code; `polarRasterChain.test.js`). A first version that kept the cache inside `writePolarSegment` crossed the 460-byte inline limit and lost 7–14 % in the kernel; the stamping now has one call site and inlines. Kernel, 7 rounds: island collect +16.4 % (49/49), large +6.8 % (44/49). Compound scene, 4 pairs: **`ringSegments=64` pre-render 5.03 → 3.97 ms (−20.8 %, 4/4)**; `boxes=60` −7.2 % (3/4).

**Polar merge by arc scatter, E-43 (2026-10-21):** kept (`43b5bc2c`; knob `2f6880df`; no input copy `fcdec953`). `clipVisibilityToPolar` tested every sweep edge against every output ray. Each edge now tests only the rays inside the arc it subtends from the light (with raySegT's 1e-4 slack, padded 1e-6 rad); an edge near a half turn is tested by every ray. A minimum is order-free and skipped rays would get Infinity, so the polygon is bit-identical (`polarMergeScatter.test.js`, against a copy of the old scan). Kernel, 7 rounds, checksums equal: **merge +1451 %**, placeholder circle +72.7 % / +18.1 % (512 / 128), so E-41's path is removed. `CompoundVisPolyStressScene?boxes=60`, 4 ABAB pairs: **pre-render 11.61 → 3.46 ms (−69.8 %, 4/4)**; default scene −11.5 % (under the floor). Dropping the input copy afterwards: kernel ties, checksums equal. Scene `boxes=60`, 4 pairs: pre-render +1.96 % (2/4), a tie; no worker 3 % worse.

**Pixi circle fan table, E-60 (2026-10-21):** kept (`7c301240`; knob `1d91efac`). `_renderOccluderSelfLit` called `cos/sin` of the same 16 fan angles for every lit circle occluder and every light. A constructor table built with the loop's own expression gives the same doubles (16/16). `VisPolyStressScene?occluderShape=circle` (700 round occluders, 360 lights), 4 ABAB pairs: **renderer 6.49 → 5.61 ms (−13.2 %, 4/4)**, pre-render −2.2 %.

**Lazy polar bin clear, E-44 (2026-10-21):** screened, under the bar. Every raycasted light fills 512 floats; ~50–100 ns each, so the visPoly row's 360 lights spend ~18–36 µs a frame against a ~5 ms pre-render (under 1 %). A lazy clear on the first stamp would be exact but cannot reach 3 %. Not done.

**Ease front under the inline limit, E-59 (2026-10-21):** kept (`7e56612e`; tools `e79834c1`). `applyParticleEase` was 717 bytes of bytecode, over TurboFan's 460-byte inline limit, so `--trace-turbo-inlining` showed it refused in `updateParticlePhysicsBuffers` and `Tween.tick`. The new front is 245 bytes with literal case labels for LERP and the six polynomial curves, and EXPO, BACK and BOUNCE moved to `applyRareEase` (451 bytes), which V8 still inlines with mixed ids. Kernel `particleIntegrateMicrobench`, three new cases (2048 particles with alpha and scale tweened), 9 alternating rounds against `e3524a0b`, checksums equal on every case: LERP 10,024 → 12,802 steps/s (+27.7 %, 81/81), QUAD_OUT 10,124 → 13,021 (+28.6 %, 81/81), 14 ids mixed 9,654 → 12,451 (+29.0 %, 81/81), rotation tween 10,175 → 12,370 (+21.6 %, 81/81), untweened a tie. Minor GCs per 1000 steps fell from 8 / 16 / 15 to 0 / 1 / 0. `tweenPoolMicrobench` (65,535 LERP tweens) 535 → 708 ticks/s (+32.4 %, 72/81 cross-round wins). Scene, 4 ABAB pairs, headless, `--src`: `ParticleIntegrateStressScene ?tween=1` particle **4.62 → 4.12 ms (−10.3 %, 4/4)**, the verdict; every other worker ties or improves. `TweenStressScene` logic0 2.36 → 1.95 ms (−15.9 %, 4/4), screening only because the baseline is under the 3 ms floor and 65,535 tweens is already the 16-bit entity maximum. Predator hygiene, 4 pairs: particle +3.4 % (2/4, under its floor), logic −9.7 % and −2.9 %, physics +4.8 % (3/4) on a worker the change does not touch (an A/A sitting with the same harness gave physics +2.85 %, 3/4). The planned primary scene was wrong: `ParticleIntegrateStressScene` spawned untweened particles and never ran the ease, so it got `?tween=1` (off by default). A parity test holds every id against the old single switch bit for bit. **Kept.**

**Compound outline per-entity revision, E-58 (2026-10-21):** dropped, no product win after G1. A per-entity `entityRevision` (Uint32 per entity, appended after the pool revision, bumped by `replaceForEntityFlat` and `_freeFixtureChain`, never reset) keyed the three `ensureCompoundSolid` call sites. Tests showed one remesh leaving the other bodies' cached outlines alone and the suite was green. `CompoundVisPolyStressScene`, `remeshEvery=1`, 4 ABAB pairs against `66b82a5a`. 36-triangle islands: pre-render 6.54 ms, **−0.8 % (1/4), tie**; renderer −5.8 % (0/4) on a 1.7 ms baseline under the floor. 128-triangle islands (`ringSegments=64`, new knob `df614bbf`): pre-render 10.94 ms, **+0.8 % (2/4), tie**; renderer +3.4 % (3/4) at 2.0 ms, under the floor. 256 triangles saturates pre-render (19 ms, 116 % load) on the visibility sweep, so it cannot judge this. G1 already made a rebuild 12–18× cheaper and pre-render only outlines bodies inside a light's reach. The patch was not kept. Reopen only with a scene where rebuilds show in a profile (many large lit islands, one cut per frame, primary at least 3 ms).

**No-pool Box2D runtime against the 4-pthread pool, E-53 (2026-10-21):** answered, no cost, tie. The pool is JS-only glue, so the same tree and the same `box2dWasm.wasm` ran both ways, with `c9ba6f6f`'s glue diff reverse-applied for the pool side. Balls, 4 Box2D workers, 8 ABAB pairs, A = no pool (HEAD), B = pool: physics **5.90 vs 5.93 ms (+1.4 % for the pool, 5/8)**. Load identical (9,004 bodies, 9,000 awake). The 5–8 % gap against E-04b was the two days of commits between them. Nothing changed; the glue went back. A re-run of the worker sweep on 2026-10-20 (Balls, headless, `--src`, stats off, 2 rounds) gave physics 10.68/13.76, 8.10/8.03, 6.89/6.39, 6.22/5.98 ms at 1/2/3/4 workers, load 9004/9000, so `4` is still best.

**Particle lights past the cap, E-52 (2026-10-21):** fixed (`7aecfd0e`; kernel `c18ac7fa`). `packParticleLights` took lit particles in active-list order until `particleLights.max`, so a bright impact spark could drop out behind dim beam particles. When the main loop fills the cap with candidates left, the rest go through their own copy of the per-light work, and each candidate brighter than the dimmest packed light (screen radius times alpha, through a min-heap) replaces it. The main loop is unchanged, so the under-cap path is the same code. Kernel, 9 rounds: under the cap a tie (+0.9 %). The laser shape (600 dim lights and 8 sparks over a 256 cap) costs **3.9 → 14.5 µs**, mostly per-candidate work for the rest of the list. A 1.5× replace threshold saved only 2 µs and lost exact selection, so it was dropped. Terrain demo with the laser held: renderer 0.71 ms, under the floor, so no verdict; it read +7 %, with spatial and pre-render (untouched) +4–5 % on the same side. Tests: sparks after 300 dim lights are kept; a filtered or off-screen bright light is not. **Kept** as a correctness fix with a measured kernel cost.

**Turned-camera cull for particles, E-51b (2026-10-21):** kept (`de5096a4`; knob `652b91b3`). ParticleIntegrateStressScene `camRot=0.785`: the box around the turned view held almost every particle and the render queue sat at its 55,000 cap. The turned path tests each particle in view space. A branchy first version cost the particle worker +11 % (4/4) through misprediction (the box branch was always taken, the turned one splits ~73/27); branchless it ties. 4 ABAB pairs: queue → ~36,000, **pre-render 4.84 → 3.42 ms (−29.4 %, 4/4)**, renderer −24.9 % (4/4). Rotation 0 ties.

**Branchless rotation-0 particle cull, E-65 (2026-10-21):** screened, not taken. Kernel, 65k random particles: −16 % at 82 % visible, +8 % with all visible. Emitter particles are spatially clustered by index, so the branch usually predicts; the gain depends on the scene.

**Turned-camera cull for sprites, E-51 (2026-10-21):** kept for sprites (`f6063781`; knob `c0ad244c`). With a turned camera the cull kept the axis-aligned box around the view, about 2.2× the screen area at 45° on 16:9. `collectVisibleEntities` now runs a copy of its loop that turns each sprite into view space and tests it against the view plus the same margin; rotation 0 runs the loop it always did. Rotation-0 hygiene, `camRot=0&spacing=12`, 4 pairs: pre-render −0.08 % (3.38 ms baseline), renderer +1.2 %, both ties. `SpritePackStressScene ?camRot=0.785&spacing=14`, 4 ABAB pairs: queued sprites **30,589 → 15,997**, **pre-render 4.41 → 2.72 ms (−38.3 %, 4/4)**, renderer −31 % (4/4, 0.68 ms baseline, under the floor). `turnedCameraCull.test.js` checks on a real worker that the kept set is exactly what `viewWorldToScreen` puts in the view, at four rotations and zooms. The other culls (particles, decorations, glow, Adobe pieces) still use the box and are open as E-51b.

**Small review items, E-50 (2026-10-21):** two kept on kernels, four screened with no change. **`collectSolidSegments` order, kept** (`64d0caa9`; kernel cases `6b7ad641`): the facing test runs before the range test, and a back-facing edge is range-tested only until something hit. Kernel, 9 rounds, checksums equal: island +4.9 % (80/81), large outline +22.6 % (81/81). Compound scene `ringSegments=64`: pre-render −2.5 % (4/4), a tie in the good direction. **Body-dirty list duplicates, kept** (`a18d3918`; kernel case `b4370d95`): a deferred mark is listed only when the slot's pending flags go from 0. Kernel, 9 rounds: a 4096-spawn window with 3 marks each **+21.6 % (81/81)**, the empty flush ties. `SpawnStormScene` logic0, the owner, ties (+0.07 %, 8 pairs). Physics read +4.5 % (6/8) and again worse on the fix side in a side-swapped 8-pair sitting, but physics `STEP_MS` tracks `BODY_MOVED_COUNT` (r = 0.84 over 32 runs) and the fix-side runs moved 5.8 % more bodies, outside the 5 % load band. At matched load the fix side is +0.06 ms (0.8 %) with a 0.094 ms standard error: no difference. **Screened, no change:** `_snapshotVertGen` (two whole-pool copies per MESH layer per frame take 13.9 µs at 65,536 entities in Node), the frameClock header seqlock (three atomic loads where one would do, once per worker per frame), light-splat attenuation (saves a `sqrt` per fragment, GPU fill only, which the CPU `STEP_MS` benches cannot time), and the littleCity jitter (two `rng()` per person-frame). Body-create allocations are open as E-50b (needs a Node kernel with the Box2D WASM and SpawnStorm physics as the primary).

**Save encode, E-49 (2026-10-21):** kept on the kernel (`62537602`); the browser time-to-save on Predator, the stated primary, was not run. No format change. The writer caches each field name's UTF-8 bytes (`nameBytes`, a Map capped at 4096 names, cleared when full) instead of calling `TextEncoder` per entity per field, and the snapshot builds each component class's field plan once per save instead of per entity. Kernel `saveEncodeMicrobench.mjs` (new), 4000 mixed entities, 49 alternating rounds against `89c94e18`: snapshot **109.7 → 42.5 ms** per save (49/49), encode **235.0 → 40.9 ms** (49/49), so **345 → 83 ms** of main-thread stall per save. The output is byte for byte the same: `saveEncodeGolden.test.js` pins length 51908 and its sha256, which HEAD also produces, plus a 5000-name round-trip past the cache cap. No worker `STEP_MS` moves, because the world is paused during the snapshot. The restore side (`applyEntitySaveRestore`) is still open as E-49b.

**Audio `play()` bus walk, E-46 (2026-10-21):** regression confirmed and fixed (`4f7e3ff6`; knob `a8b7c482`). Since `8af92002`, `_reclaimDeadVoices` ran before the `count >= maxV` early drop, so every play walked its bus's whole voice list. At 1200 plays per tick logic0 was 2.25 ms, under the floor, so `AudioStormScene` got `?plays=N`. At 2400, 4 ABAB pairs against `8af92002^`: **logic0 0.44 → 4.29 ms, about 10× (4/4)**. The fix counts releases in channel header word 6 (the mixer's `releaseVoice` when its CAS wins, and the host's `stop`, `stopAll` and `stopSlot`); each bus keeps the value it last walked at (tail word 6) and skips the walk while it has not moved. B1's ownership rule is unchanged. `AudioStorm plays=2400`, 4 pairs against HEAD: **logic0 4.33 → 0.57 ms (−86.6 %, 4/4)**. Tests: the real mixer bumps once per release, and a host stop frees room on a full bus (that test fails with the bumps removed). The last 0.13 ms against `8af92002^` is B1's ownership checks. **E-46b, measured and not kept:** the `claim` closure hoisted into a static method (`_claimListed`, 9 arguments) tied in `audioSlotMicrobench` (storm +1.9 %, 63/81; three-thread occupancy −1.9 %) and was reverted. **Kept.**

**Polar bin direction table, E-42 (2026-10-21):** kept (`c8db38ea`). `stampPolarBin` and `sweepDistAt` called `Math.cos/sin` of a constant bin angle, and the clip output computed them a third time. One table of 1025 entries covers both kinds of angle (index m is `-π + m·½·(2π/512)`, a bin centre at m = 2b+1, a group centre at m = b0+b1), built with the loops' own expression, so a lookup is the same double. Kernel, 9 rounds against `f404b2d2`, checksums equal: raster (`polar`) **+252.2 %**, full-circle merge +74.7 % (512 bins) and +107.9 % (128 vertices), general merge +5.6 % (79/81), sweep tie. `CompoundVisPolyStressScene`, 4 pairs: **pre-render 4.03 → 2.32 ms (−42.3 %, 4/4)**. With E-41 the scene went 6.47 → 2.32 ms. A golden test pins 12 merged polygons to hashes of the code before E-41, which they still match. That scene's pre-render is now under the 3 ms floor, so E-43 and E-44 need another scene. **Kept.**

**Full-circle merge in the polar clip, E-41 (2026-10-21):** superseded by E-43 (`43b5bc2c`), which merges the placeholder faster; the path is removed. Was kept (`634baaab`; kernel case `87528d3c`). Compound bodies `continue` before `occCount++`, so a light that sees only compound occluders got `buildFullCircle` (32 edges) and `clipVisibilityToPolar` ran `sweepDistAt` for every bin against it, 16,384 `raySegT` per light to learn "R" (`sweepDistAt` was 34.2 % of pre-render busy time in `CompoundVisPolyStressScene`; node timing 103 µs against 26 µs per light). The merge is not skipped, since that would move the outer edge from the 32-gon's chords to the circle (up to 0.5 % of R). It tests three edges per bin instead of 32: a ray from the centre of a convex polygon only meets the edge whose span holds it, or a neighbour at a corner, so the minimum is the same bit for bit. The placeholder is recognised by its vertices, in its own copy of the loop; anything else takes the full scan. Kernel `angularSweepMicrobench`, new `polarFullCircle` cases, 9 rounds against `7ba494d1`: **+224.9 % at 512 bins, +116.1 % at 128 vertices** (81/81, checksums equal); the general merge, nearest and sweep tie. Scene `CompoundVisPolyStressScene`, 4 ABAB pairs: **pre-render 6.47 → 4.07 ms (−37.2 %, 4/4)**, every other worker ties. `polarFullCircle.test.js` holds it to the full scan on 60 random lights across 8 vertex caps. **Kept.**

**Light sort adaptive insertion to merge, E-08 reopened (2026-10-21):** kept (`c53f163b`; kernel `7319ff1c`). The first E-08 fix still allocated (`tmp.slice(0, n)` made a new array on most frames), and `b69c880e` had replaced it with an insertion sort. New kernel `sortPrefixMicrobench` on the real worker, 360 lights: insertion takes 54 µs on random order, 3.4 µs in grid-cell order and 1.35 µs sorted, and the visible-light list comes from `queryActiveEntities` in entity order, random in y for moving lights. A fixed switch at 64 to a merge sort won random (+355 %) and lost cell order (−54 %). Kept adaptive: past 64 items the insertion sort gets a budget of 16 moves per item, and a list that spends it finishes in a stable bottom-up merge sort (runs of 32) through a scratch grown once. Kernel, 9 rounds, checksums equal: 360 random **+187 %**, 128 random +17.5 %, cell order −12 %, sorted −17 % (0.3–0.5 µs), 46 and 20 lights tie. `VisPolyStressScene` (360 lights): pre-render +1.4 %, a tie. The test runs on a real worker (stable-sort parity for random, ties, sorted and reversed input, and scratch reuse). **Kept.**

**Sync query wake for every kind, E-56 (2026-10-20):** kept on the batch regime. Physics answered sync queries only inside `doStep`; only QueryAABB woke it between steps. `RayVsBox2dBoxIdleScene` (one 3072-ray batch per tick), headless, `--src`, stats off, A = `e7393e27`, 4 counterbalanced pairs, `BODY_COUNT` matched: **logic0 16.6 → 2.9–3.4 ms (−82 %, 4/4)**; physics `STEP_MS + QUERY_WAKE_MS` −1.4 % (2/4, under the 3 ms floor). Single casts are screening only (baselines under the floors): 8 per tick logic −78 % (3/4), physics busy +4.9 %; 1536 per tick logic −71 % with the load not matched (the old code served 1024 per burst). Ending every burst on a 0.1 ms spin was tried first: −70 % physics busy at 8 casts, +31 % to +50 % logic at 384 casts; the step's bursts keep the old tail (E-57). `STEP_MS` alone is not the physics primary here: a batch answered by the wake left `STEP_MS` −88 % with no saving.

**One slot helper for the six sync query SABs, R3 (2026-10-20):** hygiene, kept. `RayVsBox2dBoxIdleScene`, 4 pairs against HEAD: physics −0.21 % (B worse 2/4), logic −0.14 % (2/4), load 2000/2000. Tie.

**Clear shapes past a batch of 32 (2026-10-19):** `destroy_loose_shapes` read 32 shape ids and treated a second full read as "nothing left," so a body with 64 or more shapes kept every shape after the first 32. The clear now stops only when the body's shape count does not fall. Node test `clearing 96 shapes leaves none` in `tests/node/rbColliderComposition.wasm.test.js`. Kernel `tests/bench/clearShapesMicrobench.mjs`, five alternating rounds, old wasm from `ba6c091d` against the rebuilt module, clear clock excludes the fill and the body recreate, step sample held the 3 ms floor. Leftovers on the old module: 64 shapes leave 32, 96 leave 64. After the fix both leave 0. 32 and 63 already left 0. A clear of 96 shapes (the new call destroys 96, the old call destroyed 32) is **552 354 → 256 163 ops/s**, slower in 5 of 5 rounds. That is the work that used to be skipped. Per shape destroyed the new call is faster (17.7 M → 24.6 M shapes/s). A clear of 32, the same destroy count on both sides, is **548 169 → 757 465 ops/s**, faster in 4 of 5, short of the 90 % round rule. A step of 200 shapes, which does not call clear, is **3.103 M → 2.898 M ops/s (−6.6 %)**, slower in 4 of 5. The same-work clear of 32 did not get slower, so the step dip is the relink, not this loop. **Kept (hygiene).** Not a speed claim. Report: `tests/results/clear-shapes/pair.json`.

**Compound occluder polar nearest-hit (2026-10-19):** boundary edges of a moving compound solid were each a sweep occluder, and `emitVertex` ray-tested every overlapping edge. A 512-bin polar nearest-hit of the front faces replaces that list; circles, boxes, and single polygons still use the sweep, then the polygon is clipped to the bins. Scene `CompoundVisPolyStressScene`: 40 moving rings (36 triangles, a hole) and 24 moving lights. Not a scoreboard row. Headed, `--src`, `--trace-cpu`, detailed stats off, warmup 8 s, measure 10 s, one window a side. Primary `preRender_STEP_MS` **37.876 → 6.916 ms (−81.7%)**. Both sides clear the 3 ms floor. `BODY_COUNT` 64/64, awake 64/64. Physics 0.506 → 0.522 ms and the renderer 0.916 → 1.920 ms are under that floor (the renderer rise is the 512-vertex fan). n=1, so there is no cv. Kernel `angularSweepMicrobench.mjs` times the shipped polar write on 80 segments at **36,190 ops/s** (checksum 426461800); that case is not the same work as `cases.sweep`. **Kept.** Reports: `tests/results/compound-vispoly/baseline.json`, `polar.json`, `kernel-polar.json`. Traces: `baseline-engine-trace.json` and `engine_trace.json`.

**Body-dirty flush, spawn window (2026-10-19):** the full scan of `pendingFlags` on every window close was the regression. Kernel `tests/bench/bodyDirtyFlushMicrobench.mjs`, 65 536 entities, one process, `timeIt` held the 3 ms floor. Hot path is a spawn that marks one slot and bumps it, so the close finds nothing still pending. Full scan **14 197 ops/s** (shipped, before the list) and **21 507 ops/s** (same scan copied in the bench). A list of the marked slots, and the shipped function after that list landed, are **18.8 M** and **16.4 M ops/s**. The shipped close is about 1 150× the scan. Checksums of the foreign-mark case match. One process, not five alternating rounds, and the gap is not timer noise. **Kept (kernel).** No scene. The despawn generation bump was not the cost. Report: `tests/results/body-dirty-flush/kernel.json` and `kernel-sparse.json`.

**Working tree versus `main` `1800f777` (2026-10-19):** catalog kernels `runKernelsVsRev.mjs --vs main`, one discarded warmup, five alternating rounds, median per side. Every scored row is **TIE**. Medians that moved more than 3% and still missed the 90% round rule: sway buckets +42.2% (68% of rounds), `gpuQueuePack` sprites +9.5% (64%), `publishPose` +7.0% (64%), LiquidFun body-couple step −7.1% (88%, lower is better), emit −6.4%, tilemap −6.0%, contact drain −5.9%, tilemap gid −4.7%, decorations −4.0%. Checksums matched on the printed rows. Headless scenes, detailed stats off, two windows, warmup 8 s, measure 10 s, same rev: Balls physics **5.768 → 5.736 ms (−0.6%)**, `BODY_COUNT` 9004/9004, `AWAKE_COUNT` 9000/9000; spatial max **1.142 → 1.214 ms (+6.3%)** under the gameplay floor. LiquidFun stress physics **4.619 → 4.606 ms (−0.3%)**, `BODY_COUNT` 3/3. Predator physics **8.140 → 7.400 ms**, logic0 **4.648 → 4.167**, spatial max **6.802 → 6.060**, pre-render **8.032 → 7.331**, pixi **4.438 → 4.087**, `BODY_COUNT` 17562/17564, `AWAKE_COUNT` **7707 → 8140 (+5.6%)**. The awake key misses 5%, the run is headless with two windows, so the Predator picture is **screened**. Not an engine-wide speed keep. Numbers: `tests/results/kernels/vs-summary.json`, `tests/results/branch-vs-main-scenes/summary.json`.

**Browser-tree CPU, E-34 (2026-10-19):** the process-wide Chrome sum is discarded. This samples the benchmark process and its children. Balls headless, `--src`, 8 s/8 s, one window, load 9004/9000. Physics **6.37 ms / 2.02 cores at 4 workers**, **16.33 ms / 1.40 cores at 1**. The step check passes. The whole tree at 4 workers is 2.02 cores. No Emscripten change. Report: `[reports/E-34-browser-tree-cpu.md](./reports/E-34-browser-tree-cpu.md)`.

**Box2D CPU sampler, E-34 (2026-10-18):** failed its own check. Balls headless, `--src`, 8 s/8 s, load 9004/9000. Physics step **6.49 ms at 4 workers, 14.29 ms at 1**. Chrome process CPU delta **−0.47 and −0.41 cores**. The step moved; the CPU sum did not, because it includes every chrome process and goes backwards when one exits. Not an instrument. No Emscripten change. Report: `[reports/E-34-cpu-sampler-failed.md](./reports/E-34-cpu-sampler-failed.md)`.

**Previous-pose copy, E-09 (2026-10-18):** rejected on premise, no scene. Kernel `e09PrevPosePremise.mjs`, shuffled dense index, `timeIt` floor held. **0.026 ms** at 9 000 bodies, **0.065 ms** at 17 556. Report: `[reports/E-09-prev-pose-premise.md](./reports/E-09-prev-pose-premise.md)`.

**noLimitFPS, E-24 (a) (2026-10-18):** screened, one headed window a side, `--src`, detailed stats off, warmup 10 s, measure 10 s. **Nav vehicle, E-38 (2026-10-18):** `NavStressAgent` read `this.scene?.mainFrameNumber`, which is always 0 on a logic worker, so targets never moved and the measure window saw a warm cache. The agent increments its own frame. Headless, `--src`, detailed stats off, warmup 4 s, measure 5 s: particle **8.532 ms**, flow **6.0 / 6.0, miss 100 %**, logic0 0.710 ms. The port was fine. E-11 and E-14 sit inside an 8 ms deadline, so a faster inner loop does not shrink `particle_STEP_MS` while the queue is full. E-12's logic0 is under the 3 ms floor. Not implemented. **Neighbor layout read, E-15 (2026-10-18):** kernel only, layout unchanged. `e15NeighborLayoutMicrobench.mjs`, 17 556 entities, stride 1025, 32 neighbors, separate threads, `timeIt` floor held, checksum 232433664 both sides. Strided **1.755 ms/pass**, packed plus index **0.491 ms** (3.57×). The publish still writes `entity * stride` so workers do not join on a packed tail; that write was not in the kernel. **Sprite-pack SIMD transpose, E-18 (2026-10-18):** rejected on premise, no C, no scene. Kernel `e18PackPremise.mjs`, Predator fixture, 16 384 rows, each case its own thread, `timeIt` floor held. Painter pack 0.497 ms/call, identity indices 0.287 ms (painter is 1.73×), gather-only 0.367 ms so the arithmetic is 26 % of the painter call. Mean painter run length **1.002**, adjacent steps 0.19 %. A WASM SIMD transpose loads contiguous vectors; this loop does not. Report: `[reports/E-18-simd-transpose-rejected.md](./reports/E-18-simd-transpose-rejected.md)`.

**Frame phase, E-24 (2026-10-18):** Predator headed, `--src`, detailed stats off, warmup 10 s, then 5 s / 302 frames. Not a speed pair. Primary: none — the spread of step-start times across workers, rules named before the run (≤ 2 ms wake-together, ≥ 8 ms already pipelined, > 100 ms from the main clock or > 5 % torn stamps and the instrument is broken). Median spread **0.095 ms**. Every worker's start is within 0.06 ms of the main thread's publish of that same generation. Step durations from the same stamps: physics 7.77 ms, pre-render 7.81 ms, spatial 6.45 ms, logic0 4.85 ms, logic1 4.56 ms, renderer 4.72 ms, particle 1.33 ms. The two longest overlapped on every frame. Particle finishes in 1.33 ms and does not start another frame; the rest of the 16.7 ms is the vsync. **Answered.** The idle E-01 measured is the display period, and the workers are not taking turns inside it. A capture that sampled after the publish is discarded: it printed a −16.8 ms lead because main's `requestAnimationFrame` is registered first. Report: `[reports/E-24-frame-phase.md](./reports/E-24-frame-phase.md)`.

**Contact pair key, E-10 and E-27 (2026-10-18):** E-10 rejected in kernel, no `src/` change. The kernel's own premise probe swept all **715,838,805** `pack16` keys and found **0 boxed**, range `[-2,147,450,879, 2,147,483,646]`, 25 % negative, because JS `<<` is 32-bit signed. Timing 42.15 M against 42.05 M ops/s (−0.23 %), checksums equal. A 2026-10-20 review note (M10) says the V8 premise was backwards: pointer compression makes Smis 31-bit, the sweep ran in Node (no compression), and in Chrome every key with minE at or above 16384 is a HeapNumber once it enters `_pairSlot` or `frameCollisions`. The Node result stands for Node; the Chrome cost is E-54. E-27 asked whether `entityIdWidth: 32` costs anything (falsifier: within 3 % of the 16-bit path), using the same kernel unmodified, 64 k entities: `PAIR0` pack16 plus `Set` 7,007,659 ops/s, `PAIR1` float-multiply plus `Set` 3,422,003 (0.49×), `PAIR3` packCantor plus `Set` (ships at 32-bit) 4,061,285 (0.58×). **The falsifier fails:** the 32-bit option is about 1.73× slower on pack-then-`Set.has` and 2.40× on packing alone. Cantor against the other 32-bit encoding is −1.8 %, so the entire gap is the key becoming a double. `PAIR_COUNT` (logic stats slot 16, stride 16 → 17) was added as the missing denominator; no scene. Reports: `[reports/E-10-pair-key-smi.md](./reports/E-10-pair-key-smi.md)`, `[reports/E-27-entity-id-width-32.md](./reports/E-27-entity-id-width-32.md)`.

**Pair-key comment, E-26 (2026-10-18):** hygiene, kept, no measurement. The comment claiming a `Float64Array` key was attached to nothing (`Float64Array` has one hit in `logicWorker.js`, the comment itself; the key lives in a `Map`), and it manufactured the E-10 hypothesis. The note moved to `entityIdWidth.js` on `packPair`, states the signedness fact, cites E-10's number, and forwards to E-27. Suite 1125 → 1131. Report: `[reports/E-26-pair-key-comment.md](./reports/E-26-pair-key-comment.md)`.

**Shadow stamp: E-29 timers, E-16 and E-17 (2026-10-18):** **E-29 kept (instrumentation):** `VISIBLE_LIGHTS_MS` and `STAMP_LIGHTS_MS` (slots 19 and 20, stride 19 → 21) were added; `STAMP_LIGHTS_MS` is a sub-timer of `SHADOW_Q_MS` and the two must not be added. `--no-collect-detailed-stats` reads exactly 0.000. Predator headed, stats on, two runs: `VISIBLE_LIGHTS_MS` 0.055 / 0.041 ms, `STAMP_LIGHTS_MS` 0.052 / 0.048 ms, about 0.095 ms total (about 1.3 % of the pre-render step); `STAMP_LIGHTS_MS` is about 2 % of `SHADOW_Q_MS` (0.048 / 2.059). The first run's `STEP_MS` 12.160 is a warm-up outlier. **E-16 (incremental or sharded stamp) rejected on premise** by that reading: the only part a shard could attack is about 0.05 ms, about 0.7 % of pre-render, a quarter of the 3 % bar; no scene. It also bounds E-08's `_sortPrefix` at about 0.7 %. **E-17 (cache the stamp cell index) FAIL (load), reverted:** kernel 0.263 → 0.147 ms (+79.2 %), which is 1.79 % of the pre-render step; Predator, 4 ABAB pairs, stats off, `--owner preRender`: **preRender 7.472 → 7.794 ms, +3.1 %, 3/4 worse**, the opposite sign, with the runner verdict `FAIL (load)` on `DECAL_TILES_DIRTY` 1.1 → 0.8 (E-32). The 52.3 KB `Int32Array` traded ALU work for memory-bound work. After E-32 the sitting reads `REGRESSION`. Reports: `[reports/E-29-pre-render-light-timers.md](./reports/E-29-pre-render-light-timers.md)`, `[reports/E-17-stamp-grid-cell-cache.md](./reports/E-17-stamp-grid-cell-cache.md)`.

**Load gate floor, E-32 (2026-10-18):** harness fix, kept. `DECAL_TILES_DIRTY` read 0.8 → 1.0 tiles per frame in E-05 and 1.1 → 0.8 in E-17, failing both pairs on about 25 % deltas of a mean below one tile. `LOAD_GATE_MIN = { ACTIVE_PARTICLES: 1000, DECAL_TILES_DIRTY: 8 }` mutes a key only when both medians are under the floor; the 5 % band does not widen (a draft that widened it to the key's own noise was rejected). `tests/bench/readjudicateAbab.mjs` recomputed all 9 recorded Predator sittings from `summary.json`: only E-17 (now `REGRESSION`, owner preRender +3.1 %, 3/4, cv 7 %) and E-05 (now `NEUTRAL (below floor)`) changed, and every earlier verdict (E-07, H-02 ×2, H-10, H-14, H-19) is unchanged. `decideAbabLoadGate.test.js` pins the floor, the band, the 50 % cv fail, and that a real consistent shift still fails.

**Pack and sort in pre-render on Predator, E-05 (2026-10-18):** dropped, Predator's `'pixi'` override stays. Predator ABAB, 4 counterbalanced pairs, 10 s / 10 s, stats off; A = `preRender`/`preRender`, B = shipped `pixi`/`pixi`; primary `max(preRender, pixi)` named before the run. Renderer **2.245 ms (A) against 3.542 ms (B)**, preRender 7.412 against 6.475 ms, max **7.412 (A) against 6.475 ms (B)**. The mechanism worked (renderer −1.30 ms, −36.6 %, 4/4) and the bottleneck moved the wrong way (max 12.6 % worse, 0/4). The runner said `FAIL (load)` on `DECAL_TILES_DIRTY` 0.8 → 1.0 (E-32; now `NEUTRAL (below floor)`). It reconciles the 2026-09-27 shared-pack tie, when pre-render was larger (11.8 ms). No `src/` or scene change. Report: `[reports/E-05-pack-in-prerender.md](./reports/E-05-pack-in-prerender.md)`.

**`box2dWorkerCount` sweep, E-04b (2026-10-18):** `4` confirmed, the 2026-10-01 confound resolved. Balls (9004 bodies / 9000 awake), three independent sweeps, primary `physics_STEP_MS`, load `BODY_COUNT` and `AWAKE_COUNT` (closed at 0.0 % spread). 1 / 2 / 3 / 4 workers: run A 10.723 / 7.541 / 6.411 / **5.763**, run B 10.792 / 7.945 / 6.521 / **5.795**, run C 10.610 / 7.350 / 6.315 / **5.550** ms. 1 → 4 workers is a 1.86× speedup; physics pays 5.0 ms to save workers and the largest offsetting saving in any other worker is 0.12 ms, so no trade exists. Sleeping-heavy scene (9000 bodies, 0 awake): 0.706 / 0.683 / 0.682 / 0.661 ms, a 6.7 % spread, all under the 3 ms floor (reported, not judged). `STEP_MS` was charging the old pool experiment for lost parallelism, not for removed spin. Report: `[reports/E-04b-box2d-worker-count.md](./reports/E-04b-box2d-worker-count.md)`.

**Neighbor cell lookup, E-13 (2026-10-18):** rejected by premise inspection, no scene. The function is reached only in the `else` of `skinCanReuse` and after the neighbour stagger `continue`; Predator runs `neighborTickInterval: 15`, so 14 of every 15 entities exit before the cell walk and the Verlet skin captures most of the rest. The assumed ~16.5 k `Map.get` per frame does not happen. A `spatialCellRebuildMicrobench.mjs` driving the real worker was written and deleted, because `setActiveList` did not reach the worker's view and every counter read zero. Report: `[reports/E-13-neighbor-cells-rejected.md](./reports/E-13-neighbor-cells-rejected.md)`.

**Nav counters, E-37 (2026-10-18):** kept (instrumentation), no speed measurement. `FLOWFIELDS_COMPUTED`, `FLOWFIELDS_SERVICED` and `PATHS_COMPUTED` (slots 16–18, stride 16 → 19) were already counted in the worker and published nowhere. **Sprite label strings, E-07 and `_sortPrefix` realloc, E-08 (2026-10-18):** both kept as hygiene. **E-07:** four unconditional per-row template-literal strings in `emitSpriteQueue` (read only in two warning branches) became a frozen numeric tag table and `formatAnimLabel`, called only on the warn path. Predator ABAB, 4 counterbalanced pairs, 10 s / 10 s, stats off, `--owner preRender`: preRender **6.855 → 6.797 ms, +1.3 %, 2/4 worse, NEUTRAL**; load closed (`BODY_COUNT` +0.1 %, `GPU_CASTERS` −0.8 %); physics +3.7 % reported, not blocking. `animLabelNoPerRowString.test.js` 9/9 (checksum is the warning text); suite 1084/1084. **E-08:** `tmp.length = n` on a plain Array reallocates the backing store; the scratch is now sized once with geometric headroom (`sortPrefixNoRealloc.test.js` 9/9, which caught a first fix, `new Array(Math.max(n, 16))`, that still reallocated). No sitting: hygiene by construction and the function had no timer until E-29. Reopened 2026-10-21 (see the E-08 row above). Reports: `[reports/E-07-anim-label-strings.md](./reports/E-07-anim-label-strings.md)`, `[reports/E-08-sort-prefix-realloc.md](./reports/E-08-sort-prefix-realloc.md)`.

**`logicBlockRange` allocation, E-03 (2026-10-18):** rejected in kernel. Kernel `logicBlockRangeMicrobench.mjs`; premise probe 24 calls per worker per frame, 480 bytes, 0.0018 % of the measured 4.528 ms logic step. Shipped 12.16 M ops/s, preallocated pair 10.24 M, **−15.74 %**: the "allocation-free" version is slower. Checksum clean over 147,492 exhaustive cases. A returned fresh object never escapes the caller, so V8 scalar-replaces it; a caller-owned pair adds an aliasing store. Report: `[reports/E-03-block-range.md](./reports/E-03-block-range.md)`.

**Spatial publish stat recount, E-02 (2026-10-18):** kept as hygiene, not a speed win. Kernel `spatialPublishRecountMicrobench.mjs`, Predator geometry (1100 rows, stride 2050 B, 33.8 MB working set): **1.09 µs per pass, 0.016 % of a 7 ms spatial step.** The premise was true about the address space and false about the cost. No scene sitting: at 0.016 % a Predator pair could only produce a coin flip, and removing a gate removes work. Report: `[reports/E-02-publish-recount.md](./reports/E-02-publish-recount.md)`.

**Dead `gatherInstancedRows`, E-21 (2026-10-18):** documentation fixed, no measurement. A recursive scan of `src/` finds no caller (only `tests/node/*` and `tests/bench/*`); the live gather is `packInstancedRows`. The doc comment's "45 568 allocations per frame" was true of the dead function and had been quoted in DEVLOG and the math-allocation log as an engine finding. H-02's keep was a 1.32× kernel win with 0 GC events counted on either side. The function stays (exported, covered by `gatherInstancedRowsParity.test.js`) and the comment now says so.

**Audit hygiene, kernel vs `5c986922` (2026-10-17):** the audio-thread lock, chain scratch capacity, mesh free-span rescan, texture sequence lock, chain ray local space, `setPlane` counts, `getMetrics(out)`, camera `setAngle`, channel lookup, and chain mass. No scene. Kernel `tests/bench/auditFixMicrobench.mjs`, five alternating processes, `timeIt` samples above 3 ms, same iteration count both sides. Working tree versus HEAD `src/` in `../weed-ab/HEAD-5c986922`. Checksums match except free-span (the new merge joins the neighbor the old scan left behind) and chain mass (disk mass versus 0). Medians: one-shot audio quantum 162,214 → 203,438 ops/s (+25.4%, 25/25); play on a live bus 3,641,064 → 2,232,915 (−38.7%, 0/25); sized chain scratch check 346,340,337 → 259,542,513 (−25.1%, 0/25); free span 160,294,943 → 126,540,232 (−21.1%, 0/25, different checksum); texture word union 3,458,173 → 3,034,855 (−12.2%, 0/25); `locateView` 16,150,132 → 37,513,599 (+132%, 25/25); chain ray plus pick 34,583 → 38,675 (+11.8%, 25/25); `setPlane` 557,507 → 601,549 (+7.9%, 22/25); `getMetrics` 51,152 → 51,387 (+0.5%, 16/25); `setAngle` 184,479,154 → 188,361,755 (+2.1%, 18/25); channel handle 69,299,749 → 69,949,962 (+0.9%, 14/25); chain `syncMass` 43,501,903 → 66,232,842 (+52%, 25/25, different checksum); `updateTexture` union 1,798,103 → 2,229,406 (+24%, 21/25). Kernel keeps are the one-shot quantum, `locateView`, and the chain query. The play path, the scratch check, the free, and the texture word union are kernel losses. `setPlane` and the picture upload are above 3% on the median and short of 23 of 25 rounds. No worker `STEP_MS`. Not an engine-wide speed claim. Report: `tests/results/audit-fixes/compare.json`.

**Ease dispatch, switch vs else-if vs object (2026-10-17):** `applyParticleEase` stays a switch. The kernel `tests/bench/easeDispatchMicrobench.mjs` times that shipped function against the same curve bodies written as an else-if chain and as a plain object of functions keyed by the numeric ease id. A direct call to the one curve, with no id test, is the ceiling on the single-ease rows. `src/` was not edited. Seed `0xe35e0001`, 4096 inputs, three fresh isolates per cell, `timeIt` held the 3 ms sample floor. Checksums match the shipped function on every cell (lerp `1543583917`, quad-in `1054200789`, bounce `1369305495`, mixed `69436109`). Median ops/s: lerp switch 107.5 M (9.3 ns), else-if 101.9 M (−5.2%), object 307.7 M, direct 1004 M. Quad-in switch 113.3 M (8.8 ns), else-if 108.0 M (−4.8%), object 314.7 M, direct 944 M. Bounce switch 56.5 M (17.7 ns), else-if 54.8 M (−3.1%), object 147.4 M, direct 279 M. Mixed ids, uniform over 0..13: switch 57.2 M (17.5 ns), else-if 53.8 M (−6.0%), object 53.2 M (−7.0%). The else-if chain is about 1 ns slower. The object is about 3× the switch when the id does not change, and the direct call is about 3× the object again, so the lookup is not free. When the id changes every call the object loses the three rounds by about 1 ns. This loop is tiny, so a small function can inline here; `Tween.tick` and particle integrate are large and were not re-timed. **Screened.** No scene. Report: `tests/results/ease-dispatch/kernel.json`.

**Time scale (2026-10-17):** one `Float32` on `clockData`. `Time.scaleSimMs` multiplies worker sim dt; the physics host multiplies the seconds passed to `weedjsDoStep` after the hitch cap. Wall `STEP_MS` and FPS stay on `performance.now()`. Kernel `timeScaleMicrobench.mjs`, seed `0x71e5ca1e`, checksum `3247727672`, `timeIt` floor held: **145,264,880 ops/s**, sample 3.545 ms over 514,964 calls. A Grok 4.7 critic read the call sites and returned no hypothesis: the path is one load, a sanitize, and a multiply, once per frame, about 7 ns, and 3% of a 3 ms step is 90 µs. **Not-measured** as a speed pair. No `src/` change after the feature landed. Report: `tests/results/time-scale/report.md`.

**Camera dead zone and shake (2026-10-17):** `deadzoneCenter` runs inside `followEntity` before look-ahead. `advanceShake` runs once in pre-render `latchCamera` and is added after the world clamp. A Grok 4.7 critic returned no hypothesis: one box test per follow and one decay per packed frame, about 9 ns, under 90 µs. **Not-measured** as a speed pair. Report: `tests/results/camera-deadzone/report.md`.

**100k tweens, one logic worker (2026-10-17):** `TweenStressScene`, `maxTweens` 100000, three logic workers, physics off, one sprite. Headed, `--src`, `--trace-cpu`, detailed stats off, warmup 10 s, measure 10 s. logic0 **4.200 ms**, logic1 0.012 ms, logic2 0.013 ms. Summary window from 10 s for 10 s: the busy logic thread is 256 ms/s and the other two are 4 ms/s. `Tween.tick` 43.1% and `Tween._apply` 40.5% of logic busy time, caller `logicWorker.update`. All 100k tweens target that one sprite, so giving each logic worker the tweens of its own entities would leave the pile on one worker. A slot split would use the idle workers and race the same `alpha`. At the default 128 slots this step is about 0.005 ms. **Screened.** Report: `tests/results/tween-stress/report.md`.

**65535 entities, one tween each, split across logic workers (2026-10-17):** kernel `tweenPoolMicrobench.mjs`, n=65535, checksum `3309558343`, 4 ticks in 8.657 ms, **462 ops/s** (about 2.2 ms per tick). After `logicBlockRange` on the slot array: 1 worker logic0 **2.304 ms**; 2 workers logic0 **1.426 ms**, logic1 **1.382 ms**. Max step 2.380 → 1.426 (−40%). **Audio storm trace (2026-10-15):** `AudioStormScene`, `PLAYS_PER_TICK` 800, `ACTIVE_LOOPS` 128, `maxSlots` 2048, four compressed buses. Headless with detailed stats on: logic0 **15.868 ms**. **Audio slot scan, per-bus list and quietest cache (2026-10-15):** a full bus was walking every slot up to the high-water mark, including other buses. Each bus now keeps the slots it holds, plus the quietest voice and the second quietest, in a tail of the channel SAB. A shot that is not louder than the cached minimum drops after one read. A louder shot claims that slot and promotes the second. A miss walks that bus's list only. The worklet's free sites update the list. `PLAYS_PER_TICK` was raised from 800 to **1200** and frozen. Headed, `--src`, detailed stats off, one window each side, warmup 10 s, measure 10 s: logic0 **8.806 → 0.278 ms (−96.8 %)**. Baseline is above 8 ms. The fast side is under 8 ms, which the campaign rule allows when the baseline cleared it and the step got cheaper. `BODY_COUNT` 0 / 0. Kernel `audioSlotMicrobench.mjs` storm case, 5 alternating rounds, checksum matched (drop count and the voices left on each bus): **20,916 → 261,576 ops/s (+1150 %)**, 5/5. The no-channel occupancy cases in that same run went through a bench wrapper and are not the shipped path (occ50 −10 %, occ90 −8 % there). `soundManager.test.js`, the mixer tests, and `soundSlotClaim.test.js` pass. **Kept.** Predator was not the pair: this function was 4.3 % of logic there.

Main thread busy is 19 ms/s. `getActiveSlotCount` is the top function, **20.1 ms in the 10 s window** (10.6 % of that light thread, 97 % of it the `for` over `maxSlots`). Kernel `audioMetricsMicrobench.mjs`, 2048 slots / 128 playing, checksum 128: **42,732 ops/s** (23 µs/call) and `readPlayingVoices` **49,921 ops/s**. At 60 Hz the scan is about 1.4 ms of main per second. There is no worker `STEP_MS` for that call. **Screened.** No counter was added.

The audio worklet does not appear in the trace (no `process`, `mixVoice`, or `audioMixerProcessor` samples). Kernel `audioMixerMicrobench.mjs`, checksum of one quantum: 32 voices 37,289 ops/s, 128 voices **9,527 ops/s**, 512 voices 2,322 ops/s, 128 voices inside a 2048-slot table **9,271 ops/s** (−2.7 % vs the 128-slot mix, under 3 %). The empty-slot walk is not the mix. **Dropped.** The panel stayed closed during the trace. `audioPanelMicrobench.mjs`, checksum 200 rows: update at 0 voices 5,380 ops/s, at 200 voices 5,887 ops/s. Not a worker step. Three Box2D threads sit in `wasm-function[102]` at 1000 ms/s while physics `STEP_MS` is 0.076 ms. They were left alone.

**Math-allocation audit, H-15 castAll sort (2026-10-14):** one headless baseline each, `--src`, detailed stats off, warmup 10 s, measure 10 s. `RayStressScene`: logic0 **4.906 ms**, `BODY_COUNT` 2000, and the scene calls `Ray.castAll` with `maxHits` 4 among line-of-sight, linecast, and `cast`. **Math-allocation audit, visibility hygiene (2026-10-14):** H-04 hoists the constant end direction in `angularSweep.js`. H-06 uses the first-order ε expansion for the pre/post samples. H-05 uses angle addition in `emitArcVertices` (at most 4 steps). The polygon gate, stated before the edit, is 1e-4 world units against a fixture snapped from the old sweep; the test passes. H-08 drops `%` from `normalizeAngleSigned`: bit-identical on `(−π, π)`, at most 1 ulp on `[−3π, 3π]`. No scene. The Predator trace names none of these functions. **Kept (hygiene).** H-09 (squared distance before the occluder cull) was **not shipped**: it is the same extra branch that lost 4/4 on 2026-10-06, and there is no vehicle.

**LiquidFun contact alias filter (2026-10-09):** `FindParticleContacts` walks the 9 neighboring cells without looking at the particle's real cell. Two diagonal cells can land in the same bucket and the pair is pushed twice. The fix marks only those aliases and filters the cell there. Pthread pool pinned at 4 (`-DBOX2D_PTHREAD_POOL_SIZE=4`, LTO full). The previous sitting does not count: the machine was busy (Balls reached 35 ms). This sitting, the same build with and without the filter, detailed stats off. **renderOnly particles (2026-10-06):** `ParticleEmitter` accepts `renderOnly: true` (default off). A shared `Int32` counts how many there are. If it is 0, integrate and emit do not read the column. If it is > 0, those particles age on the CPU and do not integrate motion or enter the sprite emit; the look stays on the compute layer that already packs them. Test `particleFlatGravity.test.js`. Integrate stress, one run, default: particle 2.460 → 2.276 ms, preRender 4.021 → 4.135 ms (+2.8 %, under 3 %). This is not a new GPU integrator: if the shader does not move the pose, the sprite is not drawn.

**R3 emit in entity order (2026-10-01):** new kernel `preRenderWorkerMicrobench.mjs` (the real `PreRenderWorker`, collector in cell order, checksum per entity). A variant that sorts the collector by index before the shipped loop: identical checksum, **−68.8 %** ops/s (1153 → 360, 5 rounds). The sort eats the sequential read. **Rejected-in-kernel.** There was no Predator. Same lesson as X5: memory order is not kept from the kernel, and here the kernel did not even win.

**H11 hoist of `ShadowCaster` in `_writeQueueShadow` (2026-10-01):** the in-memory variant won +6.9 % (25/25 round pairs) with the same checksum, but gate 4 against the patched `src/` gave **1118 → 1162 ops/s (−0.5 %, 44 % of rounds) TIE**. The gain was recompiling the method, not the hoist. The code went back. **Rejected** at gate 4. There was no Predator. **Re-landed in `4a7fa4c1` and reverted again 2026-10-21 (`bae431b6`, review E-45):** it came back inlined into `emitSpriteQueue` with no row. With the plain `_writeQueueShadow` call restored, against HEAD: emit kernel **+8.6 %** (70/81, checksums equal); Predator, 4 ABAB pairs, owner pre-render **−2.5 %, better in 4/4**. The inline loses.

**PH1 `publishPose` without the generation `Atomics.load` when nothing respawned (2026-10-01):** a shared `Int32` (`bodyGenEpoch`) rises in `bumpBodyGeneration`. If it matches the value already seen and the previous frame skipped no body, the publish does not do the per-body load. If it skips (new generation, body not recreated yet), it does not latch the epoch: the next frame stays on the slow path. In-memory kernel +135.9 %. Gate 4 against the `weedjsPost.js` from before the loop: **3234 → 7992 ops/s (+147.1 %, 100 % of rounds)**, identical checksum. Test `publishPoseEpoch.test.js`. Predator ABAB 8 pairs, owner physics: **8.741 → 8.751 ms (−0.8 %, 4/8 worse, cv 6/13 %)**. Logic −0.6 %, spatial −2.7 %, renderer +0.8 %, particle −4.9 % (2/8, does not block), pre-render −1.5 %. Load: `BODY_COUNT` +0.1 %, `GPU_CASTERS` −0.6 %. **Kept (kernel, product-neutral).** Predator physics does not move: the step is still inside the wasm. The closing trace (`after-t2`) still shows the `Atomics.load` at 41 % of `publishPose`: Predator respawns all the time, the epoch does not stay latched, and the fast path is not the one the scene runs. Numbers in `tests/results/predator-abab/PH1/summary.json`.

**E1 4-frame cache in `findACivilianToShoot` (2026-10-01):** it is demo, not an engine API. 8 pairs: logic −4.9 % but only 5/8 better. Extended to 16, like G1: **logic −0.1 % (8/16 worse)**. Particle +3.2 % with 10/16 worse, does not block. **NEUTRAL.** The cache changes who the soldier shoots for a few frames and it does not pay. The file went back to git. Not kept.

**P2 sway without `Math.sin` (2026-10-01):** Predator spawns 20000 blades of grass with `swayFrequency: 1 + rng() * 2`. A sample of 20000 floats like that has **19979** distinct `float32` values. The sine-of-a-sum identity does not apply. **Not-measured** (not worth it). The sine was not touched.

**Sway with a grid of 100 frequencies (2026-10-01):** the sine that costs is one per blade (`swayBaseAngle * freq + i * 0.1`). The later `Math.cos`/`Math.sin` almost never runs: the grass amplitude is ≤ 0.08. Quantizing the frequency alone is not enough, because the phase `i * 0.1` is still distinct. Kernel in the bench (tolerance 1e-3 against `Math.sin`, checksum of the pass): n=20000, base 1571 ops/s; LUT 1024 +15.6 % (not implemented); 32 frequencies +161.3 %; 100 frequencies +161.1 %. Tie between 32 and 100, 100 stays. `sin(i*0.1)` and `cos(i*0.1)` are built once; per frame there are 100 sines and the loop is a product. Config `particle.swayFrequencyBuckets` default 0: the other scenes stay on `Math.sin`. Predator turns it on and the spawn writes the grid `1 + bucket * (2/99)`. Gate 4 against the previous worker: **1624 → 2194 ops/s (+35.1 %, 100 % of rounds)**, within 1e-3 of the sine. Test `swayBuckets.test.js`. Predator ABAB 8 pairs, owner particle: **2.027 → 1.813 ms (−10.0 %, 1/8 worse)**. Logic −2.4 %, spatial 0 %, renderer −0.6 %, pre-render −0.1 %. Physics +3.6 % with 5/8 worse (6/8 is required for a regression). Load: `BODY_COUNT` −0.0 %, `GPU_CASTERS` −0.8 %. **Kept (product).** Numbers in `tests/results/predator-abab/SWAY/summary.json`.

**P3 sleeping cells opt-in (2026-10-01):** `updateCellSleepingStates` is 12 % of particle, and particle is at ~2 ms. The ceiling is a few tenths of a millisecond, not the bottleneck (pre-render ~8 ms). The overlay and `Grid.isCellSleeping` read it. **The default is not changed.** Not-measured as a speed keep.

**P1 pthread pool 2 against 4 (2026-10-01):** `wasm-function[102]` is the mailbox wait, so dropping the pool from 4 to 2 (`build_for_weed.bat 2`, LTO full) was the cheap test. Node WASM tests, 51/51. Predator ABAB, A = the saved 4-thread wasm, B = the 2-thread wasm: physics **8.671 → 10.875 ms (+26.2 %, 8/8 worse)**. Renderer −10.4 % and pre-render −9.9 % (0/8 worse): fewer cores burned, but the solver falls behind. **REGRESSION.** The 4-thread wasm went back into `src/box2d/`. The spinner in `solver.c` was not touched: it is not the 61 % of the trace, and removing a thread already showed those pthreads do real work.

**Kernels `--changed` against HEAD (2026-10-01):** liquidfun TIE (−1.2 %), liquidfunCouple TIE (+1.9 %), preRenderWorker TIE (−2.3 %). `publishPose` comes out **NEW**: HEAD has no `weedjsBindPoseBench`, so the baseline does not start. The comparison that counts is the gate 4 above, not that row.

**New method: kernels that do not write `src/`, worktrees, Predator ABAB (2026-09-30):** the `*-hyps/` campaigns, the one-sitting A/Bs, and `poolFlowHyps.test.js` were deleted (all of them copied snapshots over `src/`). Kernels call the shipped function or the real worker in Node (`workerHarness.mjs`); variants live in the bench (`methodVariant.mjs` compiles the shipped method with an exact diff). A/B against another revision = a worktree in `../weed-ab/`. Product gate: `runPredatorAbab.mjs`, A B A B, 8 pairs of 10 s/10 s, every worker, a regression only if the paired median is ≥ +3 % **and** ≥ 75 % of the pairs get worse. Full rules in `HOW_WE_MEASURE.md` ("Hypotheses from the trace: the gates"). The new trace (`--trace-cpu`, parser with ms, hot lines, and callers) is in `tests/results/v8-deopt/after-h9/parsed/hot-lines.md`.

**Branch `rendering-post-merge-fixes` (c40821ec) against `main` (f13b25cd) (2026-09-30):** `main` is a direct ancestor, 24 commits back: the comparison includes this campaign and the earlier pre-render, shadow, and pack sessions. Kernels (`runKernelsVsRev.mjs --vs main`, 28 rows, 5 rounds per side alternating): spatial **+36.1 %**, saturated audio **+90.3 %**, `stampLightRange` **+40.7 %**, premultiply **+36.0 %**, contacts +319.8 % with a different checksum (the generation bugfix); `packInstancedRows` +5.4 % and decals +5.6 % without reaching 90 % of rounds (tie); no row worse; all 28 run on `main`. Predator ABAB 8 counterbalanced pairs, A = `main`'s `src/`, B = the branch: logic **5.221 → 2.985 ms (−45.4 %, 0/8 worse)**, spatial **4.801 → 3.878 (−21.0 %, 0/8)**, renderer 4.906 → 3.664 (−24.3 %, 0/8), pre-render 8.077 → 5.998 (−26.0 %, 0/8), particle 2.323 → 1.974 (−14.8 %, 0/8), physics −2.9 % (cv 26 %, noise). The load gate fails on purpose: `GPU_CASTERS` 15 921 → 12 653 (−20.5 %) because the branch brings the adaptive caster budget per light (`42bb98fb`); the renderer and pre-render numbers mix speed with fewer casters drawn, and the logic and spatial numbers do not depend on that. `particleWorker.js` did not change between the two revisions: its −14.8 % comes from somewhere else (less CPU contention or `decorationPool.js`), and it is not measured separately. Numbers in `tests/results/branch-vs-main/` and `tests/results/predator-abab/branch-vs-main/summary.json`.
