**Predator A/A control (2026-09-30):** before continuing after Q1, a sitting of 8 pairs with exactly the same code on both sides. **NEUTRAL**, no bias toward B: logic −6.0 % with 3/8 worse, renderer −3.8 % with 2/8, pre-render −3.0 % with 3/8. With identical code the paired median of logic moves 6 %, and the 6/8 pair rule is what filters it. Numbers in `tests/results/predator-abab/AA-control/summary.json`.

**G1 logic: neighbors read straight from `Grid._neighborData` (2026-09-30):** trace after-L2: `getNeighbor` 8–9 % + `neighborCount` 7 % + `_neighborView` 2 % of logic. `getNeighbor(i)` now reads `Grid._neighborData[offset + 1 + i]` (no per-entity view and no call), and `neighborCount` reads `Grid._neighborData` without the static getter. It was the half of L4 that was never measured alone. Kernel `gameObjectAccessMicrobench.mjs` with a new `neighbors` case (Predator layout: `maxNeighbors` 1024, lists up to 120, a type test per neighbor, like `findACivilianToShoot`): variant **+36.7 %** `tick` and **+31.1 %** `neighbors`; gate 4 **+28.4 %** (5/5 rounds), identical checksum. Test `gameObjectNeighbors.test.js` (against the old view, widths 16 and 32, one id 70000). Contract: `getNeighbor(i)` is valid for `i < neighborCount`; past that it reads the next row. Predator ABAB, owner logic: 8 pairs −5.7 % but only 5/8 better; extended to 16 pairs: **−0.1 % (8/16 worse)**. No worker regresses. **Kept (kernel, product-neutral).**

**Q1 logic: `copyTypeActiveListToBuffer` with `TypedArray.set` (2026-09-30):** element-by-element copy loop (2.4 % of logic) → `buffer.set(subarray)`, clipped to the space that remains (the old loop silently dropped what did not fit; `set` would throw). Private function of the module: it cannot be injected as a variant, so it was measured directly at gate 4. The kernel `querySystemMicrobench.mjs` used 2048 actives in non-shared lists; it moved to 16384 (Predator) in a `SharedArrayBuffer` and gained a checksum. Gate 4 **+20.9 %** (5/5 rounds), with lists in a SAB **+21.2 %**. New edge test (more matches than space) passes on the old code and the new. Predator ABAB, owner logic, **two sittings: REGRESSION both** — first logic +2.7 % (5/8), spatial +3.8 %, renderer +3.8 %, particle +4.0 % (6/8 each); second logic +3.5 % (6/8), particle +3.2 % (6/8), pre-render +3.6 % (6/8). The A/A control rules out a harness bias. I did not find the mechanism (spatial, renderer, and particle do not run this copy) and the kernel does not reproduce it. **Dropped.** The code went back; the kernel improvements and the test stay.

**S6 spatial: a list of owned entities instead of walking cells (2026-09-30):** `findNeighborsForOwnedEntities` walked every cell of its rows, read each `gridCounts`, and deduped with `entityProcessedMarker` (14–15 % of the function in the trace). `rebuildOwnedRows` already computes each entity's position: there it builds the dense list of those whose home row is on this worker (row from `Math.fround(posY)`, the same way the search rereads it), and the search walks that list. The per-entity marker and the ownership check are deleted. It also fixes a latent bug: an entity whose home cell was full (`maxEntitiesPerCell`) was never processed and kept stale neighbors. Kernel: variant +4.9 %, gate 4 **tie** (−0.0 %). Test `spatialOwnedList.test.js`: per-frame checksum identical to the cell walk (reconstructed with the inverse diff) over 45 frames with Predator's config, and with a cap of 4 per cell S6 processes all 4000 entities and the old walk does not. Predator ABAB, owner spatial: **4.182 → 3.463 ms (−17.3 %, 0/8 worse)**; renderer +2.8 % (6/8, under 3 %), the rest does not move. The kernel does not see it because it runs the three spatial workers in sequence on one thread; in the scene they run in parallel over the same grid. **Kept (product, and a correction).**

**X5 pack: `packInstancedRows` reading in slot order (2026-09-30):** invert the painter list once and walk the queue in order, writing each row at its position. Along the way the kernel `gpuQueuePackMicrobench.mjs` turned out to measure wrong: with the cases in the same process, the shipped function had seen sprites and casters before the clock started and ran almost twice as fast as seeing only one kind of call, which is how the engine uses it (Pixi packs sprites, pre-render packs casters). With each case in its own `worker_thread` (clean JIT), base sprites 1074 ops/s, casters 1315. That reproduced D1 in Node: the function compiled by OSR deopts on `ctx.particleCount = particles` ("Insufficient type feedback for generic named access") until it recompiles. D1a (move that store to a wrapper with no loop) changes nothing: −1.7 % sprites, +5.2 % casters. X5 with the choice at the caller (not a fallback inside the loop: that cost −46 % on casters, the same symptom as X2): kernel **+145 %** sprites; control with the X5 loop in painter order **+50 %** (half the gain is the loop without screen/snap/filter branches, the rest is the order). Gate 4 **+138.1 %**, identical checksum; byte-for-byte parity test on 10 cases. With the queue in cell order (as pre-render builds it) the kernel is still at +73 %. Predator ABAB, owner renderer: **REGRESSION** — renderer +2.5 % (5/8), pre-render **+5.4 % (8/8 worse)**. A trace with X5 shows `packRowsInSlotOrder` at 35.9 % of Pixi, almost what `packInstancedRows` had (38 %): the path runs and it is not faster in the browser. Likely explanation: pre-render writes the queue on another core a moment before, and the cost is bringing those cache lines over, not the read order; in the kernel the data is hot on the same core. **Dropped.** The code went back. The kernel stays with one case per `worker_thread` and `--queue-order cells`.

**X6 Pixi: painter radix with one 4-byte histogram and constant passes skipped (2026-09-30):** `radixSortIndicesBySortKey` (9 % of Pixi, 98 % from `radixPainterOrder`) did 4 histogram passes and 4 scatter passes, each with indirect key reads. Now: the 4 histograms in one read, and a pass whose byte is the same in every key is skipped (in a stable LSD radix that pass is the identity); sprite keys `round(y) * 128` have a constant low byte. New kernel `painterRadixMicrobench.mjs` (catalog row `painterRadix`; foot-Y keys from the fixture): variant **+75 %** sprites only, **+45 %** with 20 % fractional keys; gate 4 **+48.4 %** (5/5 rounds), identical checksum. Test `radixPainterSort.test.js` against a reference LSD radix (negatives, `-0`, all equal, few values, z bands, subsets, 0–2 slots). Predator ABAB, owner renderer: **4.083 → 3.933 ms (−2.9 %, 2/8 worse)**, just under 3 %; no worker regresses. **Kept (kernel, product-neutral).**

**Second batch, not measured (2026-09-30):** **R3** (emit in entity order) is the same memory-order idea as X5, which did not pay in the browser because of the cross-core effect, and it needs a pre-render kernel with the real worker that does not exist yet. **H11** (repeat under the current protocol) needs that same kernel. **PH1** (`publishPose` without the per-body generation `Atomics.load`): `publishPose` lives in the Emscripten post-js (`weedjsPost.js`) and cannot be imported; it needs a physics-worker harness, and the change touches the generation protocol between logic and physics. **P2** (sway without `Math.sin`): Predator's frequencies are continuous (`1 + rng() * 2` per decoration), so a sine per distinct frequency does not apply; a polynomial sine changes the values in the last digit and is a visual-quality decision, not a measurement. **Not-measured.**

**Kernels against a revision: rounds and a verdict by ranges (2026-09-30):** at the close of the campaign, `runKernelsVsRev.mjs --vs HEAD --changed` marked `gpuQueuePack` **WORSE (−5.0 %)**, and again **−11.9 %** on a repeat, with the same code on both sides (`gpuQueueLayout.js` unchanged, no imports, identical checksum). Five processes in a row of the same tree gave 1875 to 2128 ops/s: one process per side is not enough for the 3 %. It is now 5 rounds per side with the order alternating, the median, and the verdict also requires that KEEP win (or lose) at least 90 % of the 25 round-against-round comparisons. With that, the same case gives 56 %, a tie. The gate 4s of this campaign were run with a single pass: L1 +215.9 %, L2 +73.3 %, L3 +51.5 %, S4 +22.6 %, S3 +12.9 %, and X1 +38.9 % are above that spread; L4 (+8.5 %) is not, but it was dropped because of Predator.

**Bug: contacts with mismatched gens in (max, min) order (2026-09-30):** `_applyContactBegin` stored `genA`/`genB` in the order of the Box2D event and the stay compared them against `(minE, maxE)`. If the event arrived (greater, lesser) and the two bodies had different generations (one respawned), the pair was purged while it was still in contact: no `onCollisionStay`, no `onCollisionExit`, `isCollidingWith` false. Fix: they are stored in (min, max) order on begin and compared the same way on end. Test `tests/node/contactGenOrder.test.js`. **Kept (correction).**

**L1 contacts: a dense pair table instead of a `Set` + two `Map`s (2026-09-30):** trace after-H9: the contact stay was ~34 % of logic's busy time (`_gensMatch` 29.8 %, hot line = the two `Map.get`s). A `Map` from key to slot and `Uint32Array` columns (min, max, gen of min, gen of max), deleted by swap; the stay walks columns (no `Set` iteration, no unpack, one hash per begin/end, none on the stay except `beginSet`). This is not "Stay without sqrt" (2026-09-21, kernel +553 % / scene +20 %): that kernel measured only the scan; this one runs drain + begin/end + stay on the real `LogicWorker` with Predator's contact pairs. Kernel `contactsMicrobench.mjs`: bench variant **+84 %** (7800 pairs) and **+179 %** (dense); against patched `src/` (gate 4) **+215.9 %** dense. Parity `contactPairParity.test.js` (Set + two Map reference, 120 frames, 2 workers of 3). Predator ABAB 8 pairs, owner logic: **6.610 → 4.116 ms (−36.7 %, 0/8 pairs worse)**; spatial −1.1 %, physics −6.4 %, pre-render −5.9 %; renderer +5.8 % and particle +3.6 % with 5/8 pairs worse (noise, they do not block). Load: `BODY_COUNT` −0.6 %, `GPU_CASTERS` −1.3 %. `contactGensPack.test.js` now pins the two `Uint32Array` columns (still no BigInt and no Float64). **Kept (product).** Numbers in `tests/results/predator-abab/L1/summary.json`.

**L2 contacts: each logic worker validates only its own pairs on the stay (2026-09-30):** trace after-campaign (with L1, L3, S4, S3, X1): `_processBox2dCollisionCallbacks` is still 19.1 % of logic's busy time. The 3 logic workers validated **every** pair every frame (two generation `Atomics.load`s + `Transform.active`) but each one fires callbacks only for pairs with `minE % workers === index`: two thirds of the work was so that `isCollidingWith` would answer correctly. Foreign pairs now go to a second table (same columns, a negative value in the `Map`) and are validated when `frameCollisions.has(key)` asks for them, plus a sweep every 30 frames that drops the old ones so the table does not grow. `frameCollisions` becomes a view with `has()` (the only use in the engine and in the demos). Kernel `contactsMicrobench.mjs` (worker 0 of 3, fixture pairs): variant **+80.5 %** in contact and **+72.6 %** dense, with the observable checksum identical (stays fired + how many pairs `has()` answers); the previous checksum used `.size`, which with L2 would count old foreign pairs that had not been swept, and it was changed before measuring. Gate 4 against patched `src/` **+73.3 %** dense, same checksum. Parity `contactPairParity.test.js` passes unchanged: callbacks and `has()` of every pair, every frame, against the Set + two Map reference, with respawns and actives that change, workers 0 and 1 of 3. Predator ABAB 8 counterbalanced pairs (baseline with L1, L3, S4, S3, X1), owner logic: **4.427 → 3.870 ms (−11.7 %, 0/8 pairs worse)**; spatial −1.5 %, physics −6.8 %, renderer −1.7 %, particle −6.8 %, pre-render −2.9 %, none regress. Load: `BODY_COUNT` −0.1 %, `GPU_CASTERS` +0.2 %. On pair 1 an audio kernel of ~9 s ran in parallel; logic gave −3.5 % on that pair, inside the range of the rest. **Kept (product).** Numbers in `tests/results/predator-abab/L2/summary.json`.

**L3 audio: `Atomics.load` before the CAS in `_writeSlot` (2026-09-30):** the Predator fixture has all 2048 slots occupied: each `play()` walked all 2048 doing `compareExchange` (it takes the cache line exclusive even when it fails) and dropped. Trace: `_writeSlot` 4.3 % of logic, 92 % on the CAS line. Kernel `audioSlotMicrobench.mjs`: saturated 3 threads **+52.9 %**, saturated 1 thread +43 %, 90 % +6.4 %, 50 % tie, 0 % −19 % (37 → 46 ns per call; at saturation it saves ~7 µs per call). The next-fit variant (`l3`) lost at medium occupancy and was dropped in the kernel. Gate 4 **+51.5 %**. Test `soundSlotClaim.test.js` (4 threads, no slot twice, drops counted). Predator ABAB 8 pairs (baseline with L1), owner logic: **4.015 → 3.778 ms (−6.1 %, 1/8 worse)**; no worker regresses. **Kept (product).** Numbers in `tests/results/predator-abab/L3/summary.json`.

**S4 spatial: a dense candidate count for the stagger skip (2026-09-30):** hot line of the trace: `candData[candBaseA] > 0` was 29 % of the self time of `findNeighborsForOwnedEntities` (49 % of spatial). Predator uses `maxNeighbors` 1024 and `neighborTickInterval` 15: almost every entity takes the stagger skip and that read lands on the head of a 2 KB row per entity, a cache miss each time. `_candidateCountOf` (one `EntityIdArray` per entity) stores the same count and the skip reads it there. Kernel `spatialWorkerMicrobench.mjs` (3 real `SpatialWorker`s, Predator config, 16.5k entities): variant **+24.0 %**; gate 4 against patched `src/` **+22.6 %** with an identical checksum (neighbor sets + counters). Test `spatialCandidateCount.test.js`. Predator ABAB 8 counterbalanced pairs, owner spatial: **6.119 → 5.133 ms (−13.4 %, 0/8 worse)**; logic +1.2 % (5/8, noise), pre-render −4.7 %, physics −5.2 %. **Kept (product).** A first sitting with a fixed A→B order gave spatial −7.0 % (0/8) but every other worker +2 to +6 % in 5/8 pairs, logic +4.5 % in 6/8: the drift inside the pair always landed on B. That is why the runner now counterbalances (AB, BA, …); that sitting stays in `tests/results/predator-abab/S4-fixed-order/`. In the same kernel, S1 (counters out of the loop) gave +2.6 % and S2 (`Grid` statics to locals) +2.7 %: **rejected-in-kernel** (under 3 %), they did not go in.

**S3 spatial: inline circle bounds in `rebuildOwnedRows` (2026-09-30):** the three spatial workers compute `getColliderBounds` for every entity before seeing whether they touch their rows (13 % of spatial with `rebuildOwnedRows`). For circles the math goes inline with local columns (the same formula as the circle branch of `getColliderBounds`); the other shapes still call the function. This is not the row prefilter (dropped 2026-09-21, +8.7 %): no entity is skipped. Kernel variant **+19.9 %** (on top of S4); gate 4 **+12.9 %**, identical checksum. Test `spatialCircleBounds.test.js` (latched position and extent = `getColliderBounds`, with offsets). Predator ABAB 8 counterbalanced pairs, owner spatial: 5.850 → 5.294 ms (paired median −8.7 %, but only 5/8 pairs better); no worker regresses. **Kept (kernel, product-neutral).** Numbers in `tests/results/predator-abab/S3/summary.json`.

**X1 pixi: premultiply decal tiles as `Uint32` words (2026-09-30):** `copyPremultiplyRgba` was 12 % of Pixi's busy time. Decal tiles are almost all transparent texels with opaque cores: those are copied or zeroed as one word; only a partial alpha does the per-channel math. Little-endian and views aligned to 4 bytes; otherwise the previous path. Kernel `premultiplyMicrobench.mjs` (16 tiles of 128², a decal mix) variant **+44.4 %**, gate 4 **+38.9 %**, identical checksum. Test `premultiplyParity.test.js` (all 65 536 channel × alpha combinations against the per-channel formula, and unaligned views). Predator ABAB 8 pairs, owner renderer: 4.146 → 4.260 ms (paired median −0.8 %, 4/8 worse): Predator does not upload tiles every frame. No worker regresses. **Kept (kernel, product-neutral).**

**L4 logic: a component cache with one shape per class (2026-09-30):** `get` (component getter, 1.7 % of logic) uses one function for every component and class, over `_componentCache` objects whose shape depends on the access order. Variant: the cache starts with every key of the class at `null`, and `neighborCount` reads `Grid._neighborData` without the static getter. Kernel `gameObjectAccessMicrobench.mjs` (real GameObjects, 3 classes) variant **+7.9 %**; gate 4 **+8.5 %**, identical checksum. (A first gate 4 gave +1.8 % but it did not count: the "before" copy was saved in parallel with the edit and already carried half the change. The copies from before each hypothesis are now saved in their own step and checked.) Predator ABAB 8 counterbalanced pairs, owner logic: **4.152 → 4.280 ms (+4.4 %, 6/8 pairs worse)**; the rest does not move. The kernel won and the product loses: with Predator's real classes the pre-populated cache does not pay. **Dropped.** The code went back. Numbers in `tests/results/predator-abab/L4/summary.json`.

**R1 stamp: `takeClosest` on (dist, idx) pairs in place (2026-09-30):** the same quickselect + quicksort, the same pivots, but swapping dist and idx together instead of sorting a permutation read with `dist[order[i]]`. The same exact result (checksum of kept ids in order). Kernel `shadowStampMicrobench.mjs` with the fixture's casters and lights: `stampLightRange` **−4.1 %**, `takeClosest` **−5.8 %**. Moving two arrays costs more than the indirection. **Rejected-in-kernel.**

**X2 pack: a `packInstancedRows` loop specialized by flags (2026-09-30):** a loop without the invariant branches (world space, no snap, index list, no type filter, no shadowCast). This is not the H7 hoist. Kernel `gpuQueuePackMicrobench.mjs` (16k rows from the fixture, painter order): sprites **−23.8 %**, casters −46.4 % (fallback path). **Rejected-in-kernel.** **Reversed 2026-10-21 (review E-45):** the loop came back in `4a7fa4c1` with no row. Re-measured on the fixed kernel (one case per `worker_thread`, see X5), with the fast path taken out of the tree against HEAD: without it the pack is **46 % slower** in both queue orders (sprites 3349 → 1808 ops/s cells, casters 2596 → 1399; 0/81 rounds). Predator, 4 ABAB pairs: without it the **renderer is +18.7 % (3.46 → 4.05 ms, 4/4)** and pre-render +3.4 % (3/4). The 09-30 run most likely used the same-process kernel, the one X5 found warming each call kind with the other; that wasn't re-checked. **Kept.**

**R2 emit type 0 (2026-09-30):** the plan asked to pick it by hot line. In the after-H9 trace the `emitSpriteQueue` lines are spread out (the hottest is 8 % of self time, `srScaleX[idx]`); there is no line that justifies a hypothesis. **Not-measured.** In the after-campaign trace `emitSpriteQueue` is 26.6 % of pre-render's self time (first on the thread) and it is still spread out: `srScaleX[idx]` 8.1 %, `srAlpha[idx]` 7.7 %, `frameAccum[idx]` 6.5 %, `srAnchorX[idx]` 5.8 %, `srAnchorY[idx]` 4.4 %, `collectorIndex[i]` 4.1 %. Those are the first read of each column per entity, through `collectorIndex` (grid cell order, not index order): the cost is the memory access pattern, not branches by type, so R2 as it stood has nothing to aim at. An idea that does come out of this trace, not measured: emit in entity-index order so those reads are sequential. It changes the queue's row order, so the painter's tie-break would have to be by entity id and not by row, or the checksum will not match.

**P1 physics: calls to `_emscripten_get_now` (2026-09-30):** the trace shows ~3k samples of `_emscripten_get_now` (import `a.b`) on the physics threads and three Box2D pthreads at 100 % inside `wasm-function[102]`. `physicsImportCallsMicrobench.mjs` counts the calls per import in `step_world` with 16.5k circles from the fixture, a single-thread world in Node: **0 calls** to `a.b` per step. The cost comes from the pthread pool (the task system's busy wait), not from the solver. The fix is in the sibling Box2D build repo: it was not touched. **Not-measured** (diagnosis).

**H6 persist: local `_persistCount` in `_rememberType0Set` (2026-09-30):** deopt #1 of Report 3 (506 named access) and 269 samples post-H9. `const persistCount = this._persistCount` and the store off `this`. One store per frame, not the loop. Headed Predator, 2×10 s/10 s, detailed stats off, `--src`, no query, against H9 (`after-h9-median.json`, 6.539 ms). Primary `max(preRender, renderer)`: **6.539 → 8.243 ms (+26.1 %)**. Pre-render samples 7.487 and 9.000 (cv 13 %). Renderer 4.313 → 5.158 (**+19.6 %**). `VISIBLE_ENTITIES` 12986 → 13105 (+0.9 %). `GPU_CASTERS` 12413 → 12801 (+3.1 %). `GPU_SHADOW_LIGHTS` 46.3 → 47.3 (+2.1 %). `BODY_COUNT` 16560 → 16633. Load within 5 %. Physics AWAKE cv 63 %: combat, not evidence. **Dropped.** The code went back. H7/H10/H11 had already shown that caching named access does not move this primary. Numbers in `tests/results/v8-deopt/after-h6-median.json`. Report: `tests/results/v8-deopt/informe-9.md`.

**H11 emit: hoist `ShadowCaster` / the queue in `_writeQueueShadow` (2026-09-30):** the post-H9 flame left `_writeQueueShadow` at 814 samples (every type 0). `scActive` / height / offsets and the queue columns were resolved once in `_emitRef`, and the helper only indexed typed arrays. No new fields on the worker (H10 had already shown that hurts). This is not H7 (pack). Headed Predator, 2×10 s/10 s, detailed stats off, `--src`, no query, against H9 (`after-h9-median.json`, 6.539 ms). Primary `max(preRender, renderer)`: **6.539 → 7.916 ms (+21.1 %)**. Pre-render samples 7.748 and 8.085 (cv 3.0 %). Renderer 4.313 → 4.787 (**+11.0 %**). `VISIBLE_ENTITIES` 12986 → 13058 (+0.6 %). `GPU_CASTERS` 12413 → 12566 (+1.2 %). `GPU_SHADOW_LIGHTS` 46.3 → 46.6. `BODY_COUNT` 16560 → 16628. Load within 5 %. **Dropped.** The code went back. Numbers in `tests/results/v8-deopt/after-h11-median.json`. Report: `tests/results/v8-deopt/informe-8.md`.

**H10 pose: cache typed pose/foot-Y columns once per frame (2026-09-30):** the post-H9 flame had `_displayPose` (1693) and `_spriteSortFootY` (774) in the collect. Views (`_poseX`, `SpriteRenderer.anchor*`, `frameWidth`, …) were latched in `_latchPose` and the hot path reread them from `this._poseCol*` / `this._foot*`. This is not the cull bounds hoist (dropped 2026-09-28). Headed Predator, 2×10 s/10 s, detailed stats off, `--src`, no query, against H9 (`after-h9-median.json`, 6.539 ms). Primary `max(preRender, renderer)`: **6.539 → 8.850 ms (+35.3 %)**. Pre-render samples 8.953 and 8.747 (cv 1.6 %). Renderer 4.313 → 5.682 (**+31.8 %**). `VISIBLE_ENTITIES` 12986 → 13440 (+3.5 %). `GPU_CASTERS` 12413 → 12670 (+2.1 %). `GPU_SHADOW_LIGHTS` 46.3 → 45.6 (−1.5 %). `BODY_COUNT` 16560 → 16687. Load within 5 %. **Dropped.** The code went back. Same as H7: caching named access on the worker's `this` does not pay `STEP_MS`. Numbers in `tests/results/v8-deopt/after-h10-median.json`. Report: `tests/results/v8-deopt/informe-7.md`.

**H9 emit: no `fill(0)` of shadows in `emitSpriteQueue` (2026-09-30):** after the stamp, pre-render's CPU was `emitSpriteQueue`. Three `fill(0, 0, count)` of `shadowH` / `shadowOff*` (~13 k rows) and then `_writeQueueShadow` overwrites the type 0s. The prefix is no longer filled; rows that are not casters (particles, deco, glow, bullet) write 0 in the slot. Adobe already did it per piece. This is not emit object reuse (hygiene 2026-09-28). Headed Predator, 2×10 s/10 s, detailed stats off, `--src`, no query, against the stamp keep (`after-hyp-median.json`, 7.275 ms). Primary `max(preRender, renderer)`: **7.275 → 6.539 ms (−10.1 %)**. Pre-render samples 6.572 and 6.506 (cv 0.7 %). Renderer 4.221 → 4.313 (+2.2 %, under 3 %). `VISIBLE_ENTITIES` 12880 → 12986 (+0.8 %). `GPU_CASTERS` 12653 → 12413 (−1.9 %). `GPU_SHADOW_LIGHTS` 47.5 → 46.3 (−2.5 %). `BODY_COUNT` 16583 → 16560. Load within 5 %. This hyp's baseline is **under 8 ms**: there is no campaign speed keep (FAIL / no floor signal). The primary does not get 3 % worse; it falls. **Kept (hygiene).** Numbers in `tests/results/v8-deopt/after-h9-median.json`. Report: `tests/results/v8-deopt/informe-5.md`.

**H7 pack: cache `q.shadowH` / `shadowOff*` outside the `packInstancedRows` loop (2026-09-30):** Report 3 had 421 named-access deopts in `gpuQueueLayout.js:349` (it was 9) and the hot pack in pre-render and Pixi. This is not the shared pack and not pack reuse. Headed Predator, 2×10 s/10 s, detailed stats off, `--src`, no query, against this campaign's stamp keep (`after-hyp-median.json`, primary 7.275 ms). Primary `max(preRender, renderer)`: **7.275 → 7.961 ms (+9.4 %)**. Pre-render samples 8.768 and 7.154 (cv 14.3 %). Renderer 4.221 → 4.906 (**+16.2 %**). `VISIBLE_ENTITIES` 12880 → 13100 (+1.7 %). `GPU_CASTERS` 12653 → 12736 (+0.7 %). `GPU_SHADOW_LIGHTS` 47.5 → 47.0. `BODY_COUNT` 16583 → 16709. Load within 5 %. This hyp's baseline is under 8 ms: the campaign floor does not give a speed keep; the primary and the renderer still rise ≥3 %. **Dropped.** The code went back. Numbers in `tests/results/v8-deopt/after-h7-median.json`. Report: `tests/results/v8-deopt/informe-4.md`.

**Stamp: do not sort pairs by index if `takeClosest` already cuts (2026-09-30):** the CPU of the Predator trace (pre-render) had `sortPairsByIndex` (shellsort) above `stampLightRange`. This is not the stamp grid (keep 2026-09-28) and not top-K by insertion (dropped). When `m > maxPerLight`, distance decides the set: sorting the whole bag by index is wasted work. The native sort is left only when the whole pair is copied (grid vs serial parity). `takeClosest` stays on quickselect: restoring `TypedArray.sort` of all of `m` in the same sitting was **+15 %** (8.469 → 9.738 ms; numbers in `tests/results/v8-deopt/after-hyp-native-takeclosest.json`) and it was reverted. Headed Predator, 2×10 s/10 s, detailed stats off, `--src`, no query, against the V8 campaign baseline (`tests/results/v8-deopt/baseline-median.json`). Primary `max(preRender, renderer)`: **8.469 → 7.275 ms (−14.1 %)**. Pre-render samples 6.804 and 7.745 (cv 9.1 %). Renderer 5.052 → 4.221. `VISIBLE_ENTITIES` 13101 → 12880 (−1.7 %). `GPU_CASTERS` 12545 → 12653 (+0.9 %). `GPU_SHADOW_LIGHTS` 46.3 → 47.5 (+2.5 %). `BODY_COUNT` 16672 → 16583 (−0.5 %). Visual load and body load within 5 %. The fast side falls under 8 ms; the campaign floor applies to the baseline. Physics/logic/spatial do not score (AWAKE cv 67 % on the hyp). Hygiene aside, not in the primary: the `try/finally` was removed from `copyActiveSnapshot` and `| 0` was unified in `publishMovedBodies`. **Kept.** Report: `tests/results/v8-deopt/informe-2.md`.

**`rendering-post-merge-fixes` vs `main` (2026-09-30):** hypothesis: tree `354f6bd9` is cheaper than `main` `f13b25cd` on the Node kernels and on Predator headed at zoom 1.5 / 1 / 0.5. Protocol: kernels with a discard process + timed (`applySrcRev` of `src/` only); Predator 2×10 s/10 s, detailed stats off, `--src`, query `hour=0&zoom=…` (hour pinned so the lights do not drift). Scene primary `max(preRender, pixi)`. **Product "faster than main": no.** Kernel catalog (scoreboard opsKey): KEPT compute n=512 +13.1 %, sceneryCam +19.6 %, meshFillPan +15.9 %, liquidfun −7.3 % ms; TIE ray / rayVsBox2d / integrate / spatial / liquidfunCouple / tilemap / preRender (srFlags); WORSE emit −8.9 %, nav −7.8 %, tilemapGid −39 %, queryPublish −12 %, decorations −4.2 %, spawn −9.2 %, meshFillMoving −3.7 %, decals −3.2 %. visPoly kernel +10 % and bullets/decalBlit WORSE are informational. Several WORSEs touch modules this branch does not differ from main (emit, nav, decorations, spawn, tilemapGid): earlier sittings already marked gaps like that as `timeIt` noise, not as a restore. Predator zoom 1.5: visual load OK (`VISIBLE_ENTITIES` 4092→4094, `GPU_CASTERS` 3938→3955, lights 33.1→33.0, `BODY_COUNT` 16656→16642). Bottleneck 4.932→4.505 ms (−8.7 %). Pixi 3.005→2.779 (−7.5 %). Particle 1.559→1.644 (+5.4 %): it blocks the product claim. Physics −14.5 % with cv 34 % on KEEP (AWAKE 6989 vs 2167): emergent combat, not evidence. **Keep of the render primary, not a product keep.** Zoom 1: visual OK (visible 5271→5241 −0.6 %, casters 5156→5029 −2.5 %, lights 41.5→42.3). Bottleneck 6.103→5.615 ms (−8.0 %). Physics +13.2 % with cv 24 % on main and AWAKE cv 71 %: not evidence. Spatial +3.2 % (5.677→5.860). **Keep of the render primary; physics/spatial do not close.** Zoom 0.5: **FAIL / load.** Visible 9931→7780 (−21.7 %) and casters 9351→7398 (−20.9 %). The bottleneck falls 8.253→6.529 ms because it draws less, not because the same work is cheaper. It fits the bounded cull margin for zoom < 1. Numbers: `tests/results/branch-vs-main/kernels/summary.json` and `tests/results/branch-vs-main/predator/summary.json`.

**Phases 6+7+8 together on Predator zoom 0.4 (2026-09-28):** `src/render/renderGrid.js` was built (multi-cell AABB, extent = max(boundsHalf, visualRange), camera with floor/ceil, overflow if the freelist runs out, no aliases). Grid cull in pre-render with `preRender.renderGrid` (engine and scene default off; Predator only with `?renderGrid=1`). The circle stamp filter (phase 8) was measured and turned off: it lowered casters and lights. Protocol: headed, 2×10 s/10 s, detailed stats off, `--src`. The pair does not exist (sprites/shadows lost) and the bottleneck gets worse. The stamp filter was turned off afterwards.

5. **Grid + cell bands N=2 + sort/pack pre-render** (`renderGrid=1&preRenderWorkers=2&sortSprites=preRender&packGpuSprites=preRender`): pre-render max 18.823, renderer 3.046, maximum 18.823 (+55 %). Sum of visible per worker ~18461 (≈ half per band). Casters 13883 and lights 40.4 close to this sitting's baseline. Visual load acceptable, but the bottleneck is much worse than 12.082.

Verdict of the sitting: **no** 6+7+8 variant beats the Predator zoom 0.4 baseline without losing sprites or without raising the maximum. After that sitting the grid, the Y bands, and every pre-render with more than one worker are deleted. A single pre-render remains. `sortSprites` and `packGpuSprites` stay on `pixi` or `preRender`. Predator's default is sort and pack in Pixi, linear cull.

**Partial scoreboard vs `main` + knobs/z-buffer (2026-09-29):** `HEAD` `4060723d` (tree with harness/floors/albedo; `src/` = HEAD after discarding a broken preRenderOwner WIP). `pnpm test:node` 789/789. Lockstep balls/liquidfun/lfstress/particles PASS. Scoreboard `--vs main --skip-node --skip-lockstep --headed-only box2d,visPoly,steadyCombat` (resume with CONTINUE on a non-black FAIL). **Product (Balls/steadyCombat/spatial rows):** box2d KEPT physics 9.082→8.535 ms (−6.0%); steadyCombat KEPT particle 0.325→0.293 (−9.8%), logic0 0.243→0.229 (−5.6%), spatialMax 0.094→0.093 (−0.9%); spatial KEPT kernel +12.4%, spatialMax 3.496→3.299 (−5.6%). **Clues (not an explanation on their own):** ray scene WORSE logic0 +28.7% while the kernel cast is **+23.8%** (`ray.js` = main); liquidfunCouple/Query WORSE on that sitting; emit/integrate/nav/queryAabb FAIL step-floor; decals FAIL load PARTICLES_STAMPED 9.7%. **visPoly:** without albedo, multiply over a black clear gives a black canvas (`VISIBLE_ENTITIES` 0 expected with no sprites). White albedo in `visPolyAlbedo.js` + a white clear on Pixi's visPoly path. **Knobs:** `sortSprites`/`packGpuSprites` belong to the developer (engine defaults `preRender`; branch Predator `pixi`). Main Predator: `ySort: true`, **`useZBuffer: true`**, `numberOfPreRenderWorkers: 2`; this branch **removed `useZBuffer`** from the engine and Predator uses `ySort: 'cpu'` + pack/sort pixi. Predator vs main cannot be sold without pinning the knobs and without z-buffer on both sides. Numbers in `tests/results/scoreboard/*/verdict.json`.

**Kernels vs `main` with a fresh protocol (2026-09-29):** the hot paths `box2dBodyPack.js`, `decorationSpatial.js`, LiquidFun WASM/JS, and `ray.js` are **identical** to `main`. The scoreboard sitting's "Pixi gate" of compute −34% / decorations −56% / liquidfun +5.8% ms was **false**: the scoreboard measured `results.0` (n=64) right after `applySrcRev` with no discard process, and that first sample comes out cold. Invented protocol: `tests/bench/runKernelAbFresh.mjs` (warmup in a separate process + timed) and `runKernelScript` now does the same; the compute key moved to `results.2.opsPerSec` (n=512, no sweep). Fresh sitting vs `main`: emit TIE (+1.9% ops); liquidfun TIE (−1.4% ms, findContacts ~60% on both; do not touch WASM); compute n=512 KEPT (+5.7% / +5.8% sweep); decorations +10% ops on identical code (timer noise, not a restore). **Nothing is restored from main** in those modules: there is no real kernel regression to bring back. Merge blockers are still scenes (floors, Predator knobs vs z-buffer), not those kernels. Numbers in `tests/results/scoreboard/_kernels-fresh/summary.json`.

**Ray stress logic0 vs `main` — isolation (2026-09-29):** the partial-scoreboard clue (logic0 **+28.7 %** with a faster kernel cast and identical `ray.js`) did not reproduce. Invented protocol: `tests/bench/runRayLogic0Isolate.mjs` — the same branch driver (`CASTS_PER_TICK=3072`, scene knobs fixed), only `src/` is swapped, detailed stats **on** (screening: not a product verdict), headless 2×8 s/10 s. Identical cast counts (`logic0_RAYCAST_COUNT` 3072 on both). Stress primary `logic0_STEP_MS`: main 6.535 ms → branch 6.175 ms (**−5.5 %**). Sub-timer `logic0_RAYCAST_MS`: 4.695 → 4.405 (**−6.2 %**). `BODY_COUNT` 2000, `ENTITIES_PROCESSED` 2001. The branch's pre-render is more expensive (0.550 → 0.828 ms, +50 %, still ≪ 3 ms) and it does not explain a worse logic0. **Conclusion:** the +28 % was sitting confusion (noise / a cold scoreboard order), not a real extra cost in the logic worker and not a pack/sort knob change (the scene has `ySort` off). **No fix in `src/`.** `ray.js` is not touched. Numbers in `tests/results/ray-logic0-isolate/isolate.json`.

**Full scoreboard vs `main` (2026-09-29, end of the partial campaign):** `HEAD` `4060723d`, `pnpm bench:scoreboard --vs main --headed-only box2d,visPoly,steadyCombat` (kernels with a fresh warmup; node 789/789; lockstep PASS; no black abort). Harness product: **No** (23 FAIL/WORSE). **KEPT:** ray, rayVsBox2d, spatial, tilemap, steadyCombat. **TIE:** visPoly (`VISIBLE_ENTITIES` 1 on both sides — albedo OK, not black). **WORSE (do not assume the algorithm):** box2d physics +34 % (BODY/AWAKE load OK; `demos/ballsScene` dirty in the tree — suspect init/knobs before a Box2D restore); liquidfun / Couple / Query; box2dRayJs; decorations (kernel −44 % on code identical to main → noise/protocol); spawn (kernel worse but logic0 −7.7 %). **FAIL step-floor / load / ENOENT kernel:** emit, integrate, queryAabb, nav, tilemapGid, contactDrain, queryPublish, preRender, compute, bullets, decals (PARTICLES_STAMPED −20 %), decalBlit/sceneryCam/mesh\* (ENOENT or load). Product workers: Balls physics **worse**; steadyCombat particle/logic0 **better**, spatialMax tie; spatialMax stress **better**; pixi often cheaper but it does not save the claim. Merge-ready: **NO**. Report: `tests/results/scoreboard/_loop-backups/report-full-sitting.md`.

**Balls box2d +34 % was the dirty demo, not Box2D (2026-09-29):** the tree had `demos/ballsScene/ballsScene.js` with synchronous spawn (every ball in `createNewGame` before play). The scoreboard only swaps `src/`, so both sides of that sitting used the same dirty init; the earlier KEEP of the same HEAD (physics 9.082→8.535 ms, −6 %) used main's `setTimeout(i * 0.01)`. The demo was reverted to HEAD/main and remeasured with `pnpm bench:scoreboard --vs main --only box2d --headed-only box2d --skip-node --skip-lockstep`. Load BODY 9004 / AWAKE 9000, physics cv 6.1 % / 2.7 %. Primary `physics_STEP_MS` 8.047→7.904 ms (**−1.8 %**, TIE). Box2D WASM was not touched. The +34 % is not an engine regression: it is the dirty init (and that sitting's 25 % cv). Dirty `pixiWorker.js` (visPoly albedo) was left. Merge-ready for the whole catalog: **still no**. Numbers: `tests/results/scoreboard/box2d/verdict.json`.

**Fresh kernels again vs `main` (2026-09-29, post-Balls loop):** `node tests/bench/runKernelAbFresh.mjs --vs main --only emit,liquidfun,liquidfunCouple,decorations,spawn,decalBlit,sceneryCam,meshFillMoving,meshFillPan`. What the scoreboard marked WORSE in the kernel was noise or stride, not WASM: emit TIE (−2.9 % ops); liquidfun TIE (−1.0 % ms); liquidfunCouple TIE (+1.1 % ms); decorations TIE (+1.3 % ops, the scoreboard's −44 % does not reproduce); spawn KEPT (+4.2 % ops, the −6.5 % was cold). sceneryCam TIE (+2.5 %). decalBlit WORSE −9.4 %: `INSTANCED_SPRITE_FLOATS` 15→16 (one more float per instance, aligned to the sprite pack). meshFillMoving WORSE −9.0 %: `COLLIDER_FILL_FLOATS` 17→18 plus `aInstBody`. meshFillPan WORSE −13 % with `meshFillCamera.js` **identical** to main (~400 Mops/s, `timeIt` noise as iterations scale). WASM is not restored and the pack stride is not reverted. Numbers: `tests/results/scoreboard/_kernels-fresh/summary.json`.

**QueryAABB and stress floors (2026-09-29):** the queryAabb row was FAILING because the primary was `physics_STEP_MS` (~0.14 ms) and `Box2d.queryAABB` leaves the logic tick. The catalog primary was changed to `logic0_STEP_MS`. Remeasure `--only queryAabb,emit,integrate,nav,bullets,preRender --skip-kernels`: queryAabb **TIE** 16.603→16.549 ms (−0.3 %), BODY 201. emit/integrate stay floor FAILs: particle ~2.2–2.3 ms with the Uint16 pool full (~60–65 k); emit kernel TIE. There are no more particles to add. nav with 192 agents and `maxFlowfields: 16` left logic0 at 0.235 ms (the flowfields are cached); `maxFlowfields` is raised to 64 and the targets move every tick. preRender with pack/sort pinned to `pixi` (same owner as main): VISIBLE 19040, preRender 2.593→3.074 (+18.5 %) but the baseline is under 3 ms → floor FAIL, not a product WORSE yet. bullets 8192: particle 2.375→2.512 (+5.7 %), also under the floor. Report: `tests/results/scoreboard/_loop-backups/report-floor-batch.md`.

**LiquidFun / decorations / spawn vs `main` (2026-09-29):** `--only nav,bullets,preRender,liquidfun,liquidfunCouple,liquidfunQuery,decorations,spawn --skip-kernels`. **liquidfun KEPT** physics 5.070→4.647 ms (−8.3 %). **liquidfunCouple TIE** 6.076→6.248 (+2.8 %). **decorations TIE** logic0 8.667→8.568 (−1.2 %); the −44 % WORSE was a cold kernel. **spawn TIE** logic0 +1.1 %; the fresh kernel was KEPT. **liquidfunQuery** with pack pinned to pixi: physics TIE +1.9 %, logic0 +6.5 % with cv 27 % (noise). `liquidFunQuery.js` = main. Primary only `physics_STEP_MS`. Remeasure: **TIE** 12.491→12.189 ms (−2.4 %). nav stays a floor FAIL (logic0 0.15 ms). bullets 12000: particle 2.242→2.253 (+0.5 %) floor FAIL. preRender 32 k, VISIBLE 19200, 2.423→3.035 (+25.3 %) baseline < 3 ms. WASM was not restored. Reports: `_loop-backups/report-lf-deco-spawn.md` and `tests/results/scoreboard/liquidfunQuery/verdict.json`.

**ENOENT fixed and the last batch vs `main` (2026-09-29):** the kernels `decalBlit`/`sceneryCam`/`meshFill*` did not honor `--output`; the scoreboard read `kernel-BASE.json`, which was never written. Now `parseArgs` + `--output`. Batch `--only box2dRayJs,contactDrain,queryPublish,tilemapGid,compute,decals,decalBlit,sceneryCam,meshFillMoving,meshFillPan,meshFillLook`. **ENOENT is gone.** decals FAIL load PARTICLES_STAMPED −16.9 % (stamp-grid, not touched). tilemapGid/contactDrain/queryPublish/compute/sceneryCam/meshFillPan/meshFillLook floor FAIL (pixi/logic ≪ 3 ms; scenery hits MAX_LAYERS 16). meshFillMoving kernel TIE (+0.5 %); pixi 13.629→1.367 ms (**−90 %**) and FAIL because the fast side falls under 3 ms (the GPU pack keep ate the floor). box2dRayJs WORSE physics +4.7 %; `weedjsPost.js` = main; that scene's logic0 **49.879→49.900 ms (TIE)** — the ray work. `logic0_BOX2D_RAYCAST_MS` is not published with stats off, so the verdict fell on physics. The catalog primary moves to `logic0_STEP_MS`. decalBlit scene pixi **−5.4 %** (keep) and UPLOADED load OK (−0.6 %) after putting that key in COUNT_KEYS; scoreboard kernel −45 % vs fresh −9 % (stride 15→16). The 16-float pack is not reverted. Report: `_loop-backups/report-enoent-batch.md`.

**Honest floors, protocol ceilings, and product (2026-09-29):** the 3 ms floor now fails only if the **baseline** is under 3 ms; a keep that ate the timer (hyp cheaper and under 3 ms) counts. visPoly: the albedo stays in the scene (`VisPolyAlbedo`); the engine does **not** force a white clear. `pixiWorker` does turn off the empty RT cookie (multiply). Headless batch vs `main` (knobs raised where the engine allows): **meshFillMoving KEPT** pixi 13.6→~1.4 ms (−89 %, the GPU pack win). **decalBlit KEPT** pixi −7.7 %, 16-float kernel **informational** (AoS 16 is not reverted). **bullets** scene particle 6.690→6.546 ms (−2.2 %, TIE, floor OK, ~39 k bullets); the kernel −7.4 % does not score (informational). **decals** load `DECAL_TILES_UPLOADED` 32/32; main's particle 2.73 ms < 3 ms (cv 78 %) → ceiling, not claimed. Protocol ceilings (not claimed): emit/integrate (Uint16 pool 65535), tilemapGid (64×64 map), sceneryCam (`MAX_LAYERS` 16), meshFillPan/Look (the pack is skipped), nav (Dijkstra is the kernel; particle 0.013 ms), contactDrain (2048 balls, logic0 1.32 ms), queryPublish (logic0 1.23 ms), compute (pixi 0.50 ms), preRender (VISIBLE 20640, baseline 2.64 ms, +21 % keep-set under the floor). Headed product `--only box2d,visPoly,steadyCombat`: **box2d TIE** physics 7.379→7.258 ms (−1.6 %), BODY 9004. **steadyCombat WORSE** spatialMax 0.080→0.084 ms (+5.3 %, **4 µs**, noise). **visPoly** with pack/sort pinned to `pixi`: `VISIBLE_ENTITIES` 1 (albedo, not black); preRender 9.047→9.361 ms (**+3.5 %**, WORSE just over 3 %; cv 4 %). Green-catalog merge-ready: **no**. Product workers do not close "faster than main". The FAILs left in the stress catalog are harness/floor ceilings, not Box2D/WASM regressions. Reports: `_loop-backups/report-floors-sitting.md` and `tests/results/scoreboard/report.md`.

**visPoly + combat remeasured vs `main` (2026-09-29):** `VISIBLE_ENTITIES` 1 on visPoly **is not a small load and not a black canvas**. The scene has an albedo sprite (`VisPolyAlbedo`) plus 360 raycasted lights and 700 occluders; the ~9 ms is the polygon pre-render, not the linear cull of one sprite. Pack/sort stay pinned to `pixi` (the same scene on both sides; the scoreboard only swaps `src/`). Sub-timers `COLLECT_MS` / `SORT_MS` / `SHADOW_Q_MS` / `VISIBILITY_MS` stay at 0 with detailed stats off, as the protocol requires. Headed remeasure 5×10 s/10 s, detailed stats off, `--only visPoly --headed-only visPoly`: preRender 8.651→9.444 ms (**+9.2 %**, cv 3.0 % / 2.1 %), BODY 1060, VIS=1 both, pixi −0.9 %. It confirmed the WORSE: it was not noise on the +3.5 %. Keep-set cause: `_sortPrefix` was an O(n²) insertion sort over the ~360 lights (main uses native `.sort()`; this branch's takeClosest had already gone back to the native sort). The stamp list was also sorted even when `sunN` was 0 (visPoly has no `ShadowCaster`; `GPU_CASTERS` 0). Minimal fix compatible with the keep-set: `_sortPrefix` becomes native `Array.sort` (a prefix copy so pooled slots are not broken) and the stamp sort is skipped when there are no sun casters. Remeasure after the fix: preRender 8.820→8.669 ms (**−1.7 %**, **TIE**), VIS=1, BODY 1060, pixi +1.2 %. steadyCombat remeasured with the same knobs: particle 0.246→0.241 (−2.0 %), spatialMax 0.079→0.081 (**+2.4 %, ~2 µs**), logic0 0.212→0.209 (−1.4 %), BODY 204, particles ~4033/4037. **TIE.** The previous +4 µs WORSE was timer noise; combat AI is not chased. Product gate vs main (physics / logic0 / particle / spatial-max / pixi, none ≥3 % worse and some ≥3 % cheaper): **TIE**, not "faster". Headed box2d from the previous sitting stays TIE −1.6 %. Green catalog: **no** (Uint16 / `MAX_LAYERS` / skipped-pack ceilings: they are not raised). Keep-set merge: **a human call** — hygiene + stamp-grid + pack reuse, with no claim of a green scoreboard. Numbers: `tests/results/scoreboard/visPoly/verdict.json`, `steadyCombat/verdict.json`, backups `_loop-backups/visPoly-verdict-remeasure-plus9.json` and `visPoly-verdict-after-nativesort.json`.

---

## This branch (`more_micro_opts` versus `main` `0695a8d`)

Isolation campaign, 2026-09-15. Each change applied alone on `main` files. Gameplay scenes headed, 5 runs, warmup 25 s, measure 18 s, `collectDetailedStats` off. Stress scenes headless 2 × 8 s / 10 s when Balls or Predator cannot see the code. **Load counts were still gated**, so `BODY_COUNT` and `ACTIVE_PARTICLES` read as 0 on production runs. Isolation keep/drop used step time only. Do not treat those zeros as “empty worlds.”

Product confirm of the keep set (HASH + P2 + P6 + HYGIENE + COLLIDE, PACT and LIGHT removed) is a separate row and uses the ungated counts.

**Pre-merge strip (2026-09-16):** `src/` still had TICK, ECB, PACT, BULLET compact, LIGHT skip, VP cache, and dead `servicePendingQueryBurst` despite earlier “Left reverted” wording. Those drops were removed so runtime matches this keep list. `hypPatches.mjs` still knows how to re-apply them for historical A/B only.
